import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/dependency_injection/injector.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:get/get.dart';

import 'constant.dart';

/// Supported language model
class LanguageOption {
  final String code;
  final String name;
  final String nativeName;

  const LanguageOption({
    required this.code,
    required this.name,
    required this.nativeName,
  });
}

/// Helper class for managing and switching application languages
class AppLanguage {
  static const String english = 'en';
  static const String tamil = 'ta';
  static const String hindi = 'hi';

  static const List<LanguageOption> supportedLanguages = [
    LanguageOption(code: english, name: 'English', nativeName: 'English'),
    LanguageOption(code: tamil, name: 'Tamil', nativeName: 'தமிழ்'),
    LanguageOption(code: hindi, name: 'Hindi', nativeName: 'हिन्दी'),
  ];

  /// Switches language globally and stores preference in Hive
  static Future<void> changeLanguage(String languageCode) async {
    final locale = Locale(languageCode);
    await Get.updateLocale(locale);
    try {
      final hive = injector<HiveService>();
      await hive.set(languageDbKey, languageCode);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// Retrieves previously saved locale from Hive, defaulting to English
  static Future<Locale> getInitialLocale() async {
    try {
      final hive = injector<HiveService>();
      final savedCode = await hive.get(languageDbKey);
      if (savedCode != null &&
          (savedCode == 'en' || savedCode == 'ta' || savedCode == 'hi')) {
        return Locale(savedCode.toString());
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return const Locale('en');
  }
}

/// AppTranslations
///
/// This class manages the app's language translations using the GetX package.
class AppTranslations extends Translations {
  final Map<String, Map<String, String>> translations;

  AppTranslations(this.translations);

  @override
  Map<String, Map<String, String>> get keys => translations;
}

/// Helper function to load translations from the JSON files.
///
/// This is called in `main.dart` before the app starts.
Future<Map<String, Map<String, String>>> loadTranslations() async {
  // Read translation files from assets
  final enJson = await rootBundle.loadString(englishLanguage);
  final taJson = await rootBundle.loadString(tamilLanguage);
  final hiJson = await rootBundle.loadString(hindiLanguage);

  // Convert strings to JSON maps
  final Map<String, dynamic> enMap = json.decode(enJson);
  final Map<String, dynamic> taMap = json.decode(taJson);
  final Map<String, dynamic> hiMap = json.decode(hiJson);

  // Return formatted for GetX
  return {
    'en': Map<String, String>.from(enMap),
    'ta': Map<String, String>.from(taMap),
    'hi': Map<String, String>.from(hiMap),
  };
}

==> lib/core/constants/asset_path.dart <==
/// AssetPath
///
/// This class holds the paths to all static assets in the app (like images and icons).
///
/// Why do we do this?
/// - To avoid typos when typing paths manually.
/// - If we move an image, we only change the path in one place.
///
/// Example:
/// Instead of writing "assets/icons/email.svg" everywhere,
/// we use `AssetPath.emailIcon`.
class AssetPath {
  /// Path to the app logo shown on Splash Screen
  static String splashIcon = "assets/icons/flutter_logo.svg";

  /// Email icon used in input fields
  static String emailIcon = "assets/icons/email.svg";

  /// Eye icon to show password
  static String eyeOpenIcon = "assets/icons/eye_open.svg";

  /// Eye icon to hide password
  static String eyeClosedIcon = "assets/icons/eye_closed.svg";
}

==> lib/core/constants/date_converstion.dart <==
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

/// DateConversion
///
/// Helper class for all date formatting.
/// Using the `intl` package.
class DateConversion {
  /// Converts Date to "dd/MM/yyyy" string
  /// Example: 25/12/2025
  static String dateMonthYear(DateTime dateTime) {
    return DateFormat("dd/MM/yyyy").format(dateTime);
  }

  /// Parses string ("2025-12-25") to DateTime object
  static DateTime stringToDate(String dateString) {
    return DateTime.parse(dateString);
  }

  /// Takes ISO string -> Converts to formatted Date string
  static String isoStringToFormattedDate(String isoString) {
    DateTime dateTime = DateTime.parse(isoString);
    return DateFormat("dd/MM/yyyy").format(dateTime);
  }
}

==> lib/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart <==
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/constant.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/secure_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:hive_flutter/hive_flutter.dart';

/// HiveService
///
/// Implementation of StorageService using Hive NoSQL database.
/// Now enhanced with 256-bit AES encryption via SecureStorageService.
class HiveService implements StorageService {
  static HiveService? _instance;
  final SecureStorageService _secureStorage;
  Box? box;

  factory HiveService([SecureStorageService? secureStorage]) {
    if (_instance != null) {
      return _instance!;
    }
    _instance = HiveService._internal(secureStorage ?? SecureStorageService());
    return _instance!;
  }

  HiveService._internal(this._secureStorage);

  // Completer is used to wait for the box to open if it hasn't yet
  final Completer<Box> initCompleter = Completer<Box>();

  /// Initialize the database with encryption
  @override
  Future<void> init() async {
    if (initCompleter.isCompleted) return;
    await Hive.initFlutter();

    // Get the encryption key from secure storage
    final encryptionKey = await _secureStorage.getEncryptionKey();

    // Open an encrypted box
    final encryptedBox = await Hive.openBox(
      hiveBoxName,
      encryptionCipher: HiveAesCipher(encryptionKey),
    );
    box = encryptedBox;
    initCompleter.complete(encryptedBox);
  }

  /// Check if database is ready
  @override
  bool get hasInitialized => initCompleter.isCompleted;

  /// Synchronous getter for saved language code
  String? getLanguage() {
    if (box != null && box!.isOpen) {
      return box!.get(languageDbKey)?.toString();
    }
    if (Hive.isBoxOpen(hiveBoxName)) {
      return Hive.box(hiveBoxName).get(languageDbKey)?.toString();
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// Get a value by its key
  @override
  Future<Object?> get(String key) async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    return box?.get(key);
  }

  /// Clear all data (used during logout)
  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    // Clearing specific fields or the whole box
    await box?.put('user', {}.toString());
    await box?.clear();
  }

  /// Check if a key exists
  @override
  Future<bool> has(String key) async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    return box?.containsKey(key) ?? false;
  }

  /// Remove a specific item
  @override
  Future<bool> remove(String key) async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    await box?.delete(key);
    return true;
  }

  /// Save a value
  @override
  Future<bool> set(String key, data) async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    await box?.put(key, data.toString());
    return true;
  }

  /// Helper to save the UserData object
  /// We convert the object to JSON string because Hive stores simple types best.
  @override
  Future<bool> setUser(UserData data) async {
    box = await initCompleter.future;
    await box?.put(userDbKey, jsonEncode(data.toJson()));
    return true;
  }

  /// Helper to get the UserData object
  @override
  Future<UserData> getUser() async {
    Object? data = await box?.get(userDbKey);
    dynamic userJson = jsonDecode(data.toString());
    return UserData.fromJson(userJson);
  }
}

/// Alias for HiveService for consistency with user request
typedef HiveStorageService = HiveService;

==> lib/core/database/secure_storage_service.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:hive_flutter/hive_flutter.dart';

/// SecureStorageService
/// 
/// Handles secure storage operations using flutter_secure_storage.
/// Primarily used to store the encryption key for Hive.
class SecureStorageService {
  final FlutterSecureStorage _secureStorage = const FlutterSecureStorage();
  static const String _encryptionKeyName = 'hive_encryption_key';

  /// Generates or retrieves a 256-bit encryption key for Hive.
  Future<List<int>> getEncryptionKey() async {
    final containsEncryptionKey = await _secureStorage.containsKey(key: _encryptionKeyName);
    
    if (!containsEncryptionKey) {
      // Generate a new 256-bit key
      final key = Hive.generateSecureKey();
      // Store it as a base64 string
      await _secureStorage.write(
        key: _encryptionKeyName,
        value: base64UrlEncode(key),
      );
    }

    // Retrieve and decode the key
    final base64Key = await _secureStorage.read(key: _encryptionKeyName);
    return base64Url.decode(base64Key!);
  }

  /// Deletes the encryption key (use with caution!)
  Future<void> deleteEncryptionKey() async {
    await _secureStorage.delete(key: _encryptionKeyName);
  }
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

abstract class StorageService {
  void init();

  bool get hasInitialized;

  Future<bool> remove(String key);

  Future<Object?> get(String key);

  Future<bool> set(String key, String data);

  Future<void> clear();

  Future<bool> has(String key);

  Future<bool> setUser(UserData user);

  Future<UserData> getUser();
}

/// Service Locator Instance
final injector = GetIt.instance;

/// Dependency Injection Setup
///
/// This function initializes all services, data sources, repositories,
/// and use cases for the application. It follows a layered registration
/// approach to ensure dependencies are resolved correctly.
Future<void> init() async {
  /// --- Core Services (Layer 0) ---
  final secureStorage = SecureStorageService();
  injector.registerLazySingleton<SecureStorageService>(() => secureStorage);

  final hiveService = HiveService(secureStorage);
  await hiveService.init();
  injector.registerLazySingleton<HiveService>(() => hiveService);

  injector.registerLazySingleton<NetworkService>(DioNetworkService.new);
  injector.registerLazySingleton<Connectivity>(Connectivity.new);
  injector.registerLazySingleton<NetworkInfo>(
    () => NetworkInfoImpl(injector()),
  );

  /// --- DataSources (Layer 1) ---
  injector
    // Login Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<LoginRemoteDataSource>(
      () => LoginRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Grocery Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<GroceryRemoteDataSource>(
      () => GroceryRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Cart Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<CartRemoteDataSource>(
      () => CartRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Checkout Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<CheckoutRemoteDataSource>(
      () => CheckoutRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Wishlist Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<WishlistRemoteDataSource>(
      () => WishlistRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Order Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<OrderRemoteDataSource>(
      () => OrderRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Profile Data Source
    ..registerLazySingleton<ProfileRemoteDataSource>(
      () => ProfileRemoteDataSourceImpl(injector()),
    )
    /// --- Repositories (Layer 2) ---
    // Login Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<LoginRepository>(
      () => LoginRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Grocery Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<GroceryRepository>(
      () => GroceryRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Cart Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<CartRepository>(
      () => CartRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Checkout Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<CheckoutRepository>(
      () => CheckoutRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Wishlist Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<WishlistRepository>(
      () => WishlistRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Order Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<OrderRepository>(
      () => OrderRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    // Profile Repo
    ..registerLazySingleton<ProfileRepository>(
      () => ProfileRepositoryImpl(injector()),
    )
    /// --- UseCases (Layer 3) ---
    // Login Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<LoginUseCases>(() => LoginUseCases(injector()))
    // Grocery Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<GetGroceryItemsUseCase>(
      () => GetGroceryItemsUseCase(injector()),
    )
    ..registerLazySingleton<GetProductDetailUseCase>(
      () => GetProductDetailUseCase(injector()),
    )
    // Cart Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<CartUseCase>(() => CartUseCase(injector()))
    // Checkout Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<CheckoutUseCase>(() => CheckoutUseCase(injector()))
    // Wishlist Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<WishlistUseCase>(() => WishlistUseCase(injector()))
    // Order Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<OrderUseCase>(() => OrderUseCase(injector()))
    // Profile Logic
    ..registerLazySingleton<ProfileUseCase>(() => ProfileUseCase(injector()));
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:developer';

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/routes.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/connection/connection_listener.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/response.dart' as response;
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/configuration.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/routes/app_routes.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// ExceptionHandlerMixin
///
/// This is a "Mixin" - a piece of code we can add to other classes (like NetworkService).
/// It handles COMMMON errors so we don't have to write try-catch blocks everywhere.
///
/// What it handles:
/// 1. No Internet connection.
/// 2. Unauthorized (401) -> Automatically logs user out.
/// 3. Server errors (500).
/// 4. Timeouts.
mixin ExceptionHandlerMixin on NetworkService {
  final HiveService _hiveService = GetIt.instance<HiveService>();
  UserPreferences? userPreferences;
  NetworkService? networkService;

  /// Main handler function. Wraps an API call.
  ///
  /// Usage:
  /// ```dart
  /// return handleException(() => dio.get(...));
  /// ```
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>>
  handleException<T extends Object>(
    Future<Response<dynamic>> Function() handler, {
    String endpoint = '',
  }) async {
    var connectionStatus = ConnectionStatusListener.getInstance();

    // 1. Check Internet first
    if (await connectionStatus.checkConnection()) {
      try {
        // 2. Try the API call
        Response<dynamic> res = await handler();

        // 3. If success, return Right (Success data)
        return Right(
          response.Response(
            statusCode: res.statusCode ?? 200,
            data: res.data,
            statusMessage: res.statusMessage,
          ),
        );
      } catch (e) {
        // 4. If error (catch block), figure out what happened
        String message = '';
        String identifier = '';
        int statusCode = 0;

        if (e is DioException) {
          // Special Case: Session Expired (401)
          if (e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
            log("Unauthorized - Triggering logout...${e.response?.statusCode}");
            logout();

            return Left(
              AppException(
                message: AppStrings.sessionExpiryMsg,
                statusCode: 401,
                identifier: 'Unauthorized at $endpoint',
              ),
            );
          }

          // Extract readable message from backend
          final responseData = e.response?.data;
          if (responseData is Map<String, dynamic> &&
              responseData.containsKey('message')) {
            message = responseData['message'];
          } else {
            message = e.message ?? AppStrings.errorOccurred;
          }
          statusCode = e.response?.statusCode ?? 0;
          identifier = 'DioException ${e.message} \nat $endpoint';
        } else if (e is SocketException) {
          message = AppStrings.unableToConnect;
          statusCode = 0;
          identifier = 'Socket Exception ${e.message}\n at $endpoint';
        } else {
          message = AppStrings.unknownError;
          statusCode = 0;
          identifier = 'Unknown error ${e.toString()}\n at $endpoint';
        }

        // Return the error
        return Left(
          AppException(
            message: message,
            statusCode: statusCode,
            identifier: identifier,
          ),
        );
      }
    } else {
      // No Internet
      log("No internet");
      return Left(
        AppException(
          message: AppStrings.noInternetMsg,
          statusCode: 0,
          identifier: 'No Internet at $endpoint',
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  /// Logs user out if session expired
  void logout() async {
    await _hiveService.clear();
    userPreferences?.clearPreferences();
    networkService?.updateHeader({'Authorization': ''});
    nav();
  }

  /// Redirect to Login
  void nav() {
    navigatorKey.currentState?.context.go(RoutesName.loginPath);
  }
}

==> lib/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/response.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

/// AppException
///
/// A standard custom exception class for our app.
/// Instead of using random strings or numbers, we use this object.
///
/// Contains:
/// - message: User-friendly error text.
/// - statusCode: HTML status code (404, 500) or 0 for local errors.
/// - identifier: Technical info for developers (where it happened).
class AppException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  final int statusCode;
  final String identifier;

  AppException({
    required this.message,
    required this.statusCode,
    required this.identifier,
  });

  @override
  String toString() {
    return 'statusCode=$statusCode\nmessage=$message\nidentifier=$identifier';
  }
}

/// CacheFailureException
///
/// Specific error when local database (Hive) fails.
class CacheFailureException extends Equatable implements AppException {
  @override
  String get identifier => 'Cache failure exception';

  @override
  String get message => 'Unable to save user';

  @override
  int get statusCode => 100;

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message, statusCode, identifier];
}

/// Helper extension to easily convert an Exception to a "Left" (Error) return type.
extension HttpExceptionExtension on AppException {
  Left<AppException, Response> get toLeft => Left<AppException, Response>(this);
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// ContextExtensions
///
/// Extension methods on [BuildContext] to simplify access to screen metrics,
/// safe area insets (notches, dynamic islands, navigation bars), keyboard state,
/// and design tokens.
extension ContextExtensions on BuildContext {
  /// MediaQuery Data
  MediaQueryData get mediaQuery => MediaQuery.of(this);

  /// Screen Dimensions
  Size get screenSize => mediaQuery.size;

  double get screenHeight => screenSize.height;

  double get screenWidth => screenSize.width;

  /// Safe Area Insets & Padding
  EdgeInsets get mediaPadding => mediaQuery.padding;

  double get topPadding => mediaPadding.top;

  double get bottomPadding => mediaPadding.bottom;

  double get leftPadding => mediaPadding.left;

  double get rightPadding => mediaPadding.right;

  /// Keyboard / View Insets
  EdgeInsets get viewInsets => mediaQuery.viewInsets;

  double get keyboardHeight => viewInsets.bottom;

  bool get isKeyboardOpen => keyboardHeight > 0;

  /// Theme & Typography Shortcuts
  ThemeData get theme => Theme.of(this);

  ColorScheme get colorScheme => theme.colorScheme;

  TextTheme get textTheme => theme.textTheme;

  /// Standard Form / Auth Scroll Padding
  ///
  /// Automatically accounts for the status bar / notch at the top
  /// and the virtual home bar / navigation bar at the bottom.
  EdgeInsets get authScreenPadding =>
      EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, topPadding + 16, 20, bottomPadding + 32);

  /// Dynamic Edge-to-Edge Scroll Padding helper
  EdgeInsets authScrollPadding({
    double horizontal = 20,
    double topExtra = 16,
    double bottomExtra = 32,
  }) => EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
    horizontal,
    topPadding + topExtra,
    horizontal,
    bottomPadding + bottomExtra,
  );
}

==> lib/core/extension/roles.dart <==
/// UserRole
///
/// Defines the types of users in the system.
enum UserRole { admin, customer }

/// Helper extension to convert Role to ID and back.
/// Useful when sending/receiving data from API.
extension UserRoleExtension on UserRole {
  /// Get ID from Role
  int get id {
    switch (this) {
      case UserRole.admin:
        return 1;
      case UserRole.customer:
        return 2;
    }
  }

  /// Get Role from ID
  static UserRole fromId(int id) {
    switch (id) {
      case 1:
        return UserRole.admin;
      case 2:
        return UserRole.customer;
      default:
        throw Exception("Invalid user role ID");
    }
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/auth_interceptors.dart <==
import 'dart:developer';

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/constant.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/logout_service_function.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/refresh_token_response.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:jwt_decoder/jwt_decoder.dart';

/// AuthInterceptors
///
/// Production enterprise interceptor handling:
/// 1. Automatic Bearer token injection on outgoing non-auth requests
/// 2. Auth endpoint exclusions (preventing refresh loops on login/register)
/// 3. Concurrency-protected token refresh queue on 401 Unauthorized
/// 4. Graceful fallback to LogoutService on token revocation
class AuthInterceptors extends Interceptor {
  final Dio dio;

  AuthInterceptors({required this.dio});

  final _hiveService = GetIt.instance<HiveService>();

  bool _isRefreshing = false;
  final List<({RequestOptions options, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler})>
  _queue = [];

  bool _isAuthEndpoint(String path) {
    return path.contains(ApiEndpoint.login) ||
        path.contains(ApiEndpoint.register) ||
        path.contains(ApiEndpoint.refreshToken) ||
        path.contains('auth/signup') ||
        path.contains('auth/forgot-password') ||
        path.contains('auth/reset-password') ||
        path.contains('auth/verify-otp');
  }

  void setNewToken(String token) {
    _hiveService.set(userToken, token);
  }

  @override
  void onRequest(
    RequestOptions options,
    RequestInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) async {
    final isExternal =
        options.path.startsWith('http://') ||
        options.path.startsWith('https://');

    // Automatically inject Bearer token for internal, non-auth endpoints
    if (!isExternal && !_isAuthEndpoint(options.path)) {
      final token = await _hiveService.get(userToken) as String?;
      if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
        options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
      }
    }

    if (!isExternal) {
      options.headers['Accept'] = 'application/json';
    }

    return handler.next(options);
  }

  @override
  void onError(DioException err, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) async {
    final requestPath = err.requestOptions.path;

    // Do not intercept or retry auth endpoints
    if (_isAuthEndpoint(requestPath)) {
      if (requestPath.contains(ApiEndpoint.refreshToken)) {
        log('Refresh token endpoint failed. Clearing session.');
        await LogoutService.instance.clearData();
      }
      return handler.next(err);
    }

    // Do not retry if request already failed on a retry attempt
    if (err.requestOptions.extra['isRetry'] == true) {
      log(
        'Retry request failed for ${err.requestOptions.path}. Redirecting to logout.',
      );
      await LogoutService.instance.clearData();
      return handler.next(err);
    }

    final token = await _hiveService.get(refreshToken) as String?;
    final access = await _hiveService.get(userToken) as String?;

    // Check for missing session tokens or deactivated account message
    final data = err.response?.data;
    final isUserInactive =
        data is Map &&
        data['message'] == 'User is inactive or deleted, please contact admin';

    if (token == null || token.isEmpty || isUserInactive) {
      log('No refresh token available or inactive user. Logging out.');
      await LogoutService.instance.clearData();
      return handler.next(err);
    }

    bool isExpired = false;
    if (access != null && access.isNotEmpty) {
      try {
        isExpired = JwtDecoder.isExpired(access);
      } catch (_) {
        isExpired = true;
      }
    } else {
      isExpired = true;
    }

    if (err.response?.statusCode == 401 || isExpired) {
      // If a refresh is already in progress, enqueue this failed request
      if (_isRefreshing) {
        log(
          'Token refresh in progress. Enqueueing request: ${err.requestOptions.path}',
        );
        _queue.add((options: err.requestOptions, handler: handler));
        return;
      }

      _isRefreshing = true;
      log(
        'Initiating single-lock token refresh for: ${err.requestOptions.path}',
      );

      try {
        final response = await dio.post(
          ApiEndpoint.refreshToken,
          data: {'refreshToken': token},
        );

        final refreshTokenResponse = RefreshTokenResponse.fromJson(
          response.data,
        );
        final newAccessToken = refreshTokenResponse.data.accessToken;

        if (newAccessToken.isEmpty) {
          throw Exception('Empty access token received from refresh endpoint');
        }

        setNewToken(newAccessToken);
        if (refreshTokenResponse.data.refreshToken != null &&
            refreshTokenResponse.data.refreshToken!.isNotEmpty) {
          await _hiveService.set(
            refreshToken,
            refreshTokenResponse.data.refreshToken!,
          );
        }
        dio.options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $newAccessToken';

        // 1. Retry the original request
        final retryOptions = err.requestOptions;
        retryOptions.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $newAccessToken';
        retryOptions.extra['isRetry'] = true;

        log('Retrying original request: ${retryOptions.path}');
        final originalResponse = await dio.fetch(retryOptions);
        handler.resolve(originalResponse);

        // 2. Retry all queued requests with the fresh token
        for (final item in _queue) {
          final queuedOptions = item.options;
          queuedOptions.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $newAccessToken';
          queuedOptions.extra['isRetry'] = true;
          try {
            log('Retrying queued request: ${queuedOptions.path}');
            final queuedResponse = await dio.fetch(queuedOptions);
            item.handler.resolve(queuedResponse);
          } on DioException catch (dioErr) {
            item.handler.reject(dioErr);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AuthInterceptors Refresh Failure: ${e.toString()}');
        // Reject all queued requests
        for (final item in _queue) {
          item.handler.reject(err);
        }
        await LogoutService.instance.clearData();
        return handler.next(err);
      } finally {
        _queue.clear();
        _isRefreshing = false;
      }
      return;
    }

    super.onError(err, handler);
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/connection/connection_listener.dart <==
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:connectivity_plus/connectivity_plus.dart';

/// ConnectionStatusListener
///
/// Listens for internet connection changes (Online / Offline).
///
/// How it works:
/// 1. Uses `connectivity_plus` to detect network changes (WiFi/Mobile).
/// 2. Pings a reliable server (google.com) to verify actual internet access.
/// 3. Broadcasts the status to the rest of the app via a Stream.
class ConnectionStatusListener {
  // Singleton instance
  static final _singleton = ConnectionStatusListener._internal();

  ConnectionStatusListener._internal();

  bool hasShownNoInternet = false;

  // Tools
  final Connectivity _connectivity = Connectivity();

  // Public access
  static ConnectionStatusListener getInstance() => _singleton;

  // Tracker
  bool hasConnection = false;

  // Controller to send updates to listeners
  StreamController connectionChangeController = StreamController.broadcast();

  // Stream that UI can listen to
  Stream get connectionChange => connectionChangeController.stream;

  /// Internal listener for connectivity_plus
  void _connectionChange(List<ConnectivityResult> results) {
    if (results.isNotEmpty) {
      checkConnection();
    }
  }

  /// Real test: tries to lookup google.com to verify internet
  Future<bool> checkConnection() async {
    bool previousConnection = hasConnection;

    try {
      List<InternetAddress> result = await InternetAddress.lookup('google.com');
      if (result.isNotEmpty && result[0].rawAddress.isNotEmpty) {
        hasConnection = true;
      } else {
        hasConnection = false;
      }
    } on SocketException catch (_) {
      hasConnection = false;
    }

    // If status changed, notify listeners
    if (previousConnection != hasConnection) {
      connectionChangeController.add(hasConnection);
    }

    return hasConnection;
  }

  /// Initialize listener (call this at app start)
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    _connectivity.onConnectivityChanged.listen(_connectionChange);
    await checkConnection();
  }

  /// Cleanup
  void dispose() {
    connectionChangeController.close();
  }
}

/// Helper to handle UI updates when connection changes
void updateConnectivity(
  dynamic hasConnection,
  ConnectionStatusListener connectionStatus,
) {
  if (!hasConnection) {
    connectionStatus.hasShownNoInternet = true;
    // Here we can show a "No Internet" popup or banner
  } else {
    if (connectionStatus.hasShownNoInternet) {
      connectionStatus.hasShownNoInternet = false;
    }
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/connection/network_info.dart <==
import 'package:connectivity_plus/connectivity_plus.dart';

/// NetworkInfo
///
/// Abstract contract for checking network connection.
/// Mockable and injectable for Clean Architecture testing.
abstract class NetworkInfo {
  Future<bool> get isConnected;

  Stream<List<ConnectivityResult>> get onConnectivityChanged;
}

/// NetworkInfoImpl
///
/// Production implementation of [NetworkInfo] using [Connectivity].
class NetworkInfoImpl implements NetworkInfo {
  final Connectivity _connectivity;

  NetworkInfoImpl(this._connectivity);

  @override
  Future<bool> get isConnected async {
    final results = await _connectivity.checkConnectivity();
    return !results.contains(ConnectivityResult.none);
  }

  @override
  Stream<List<ConnectivityResult>> get onConnectivityChanged =>
      _connectivity.onConnectivityChanged;
}

==> lib/core/network/dio_network_service.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/exception_handler_mixin.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/main/app_env.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_pretty_dio_logger/flutter_pretty_dio_logger.dart';
import 'auth_interceptors.dart';
import 'model/either.dart';
import 'model/response.dart' as response;
import 'network_service.dart';

/// DioNetworkService
///
/// This class handles all API calls using the Dio package.
/// Now enhanced with AuthInterceptors and 5000ms timeouts.
class DioNetworkService extends NetworkService with ExceptionHandlerMixin {
  late final Dio _dio;

  DioNetworkService() {
    _dio = Dio();
    _dio.options = dioBaseOptions;

    // Add Auth Interceptor for token management
    _dio.interceptors.add(AuthInterceptors(dio: _dio));

    // Add Logger only in Debug mode
    if (kDebugMode) {
      _dio.interceptors.add(
        PrettyDioLogger(
          queryParameters: true,
          requestHeader: true,
          requestBody: true,
          responseBody: true,
          error: true,
          showProcessingTime: true,
          canShowLog: kDebugMode,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  /// Default configurations (Base URL, Headers, Timeouts)
  BaseOptions get dioBaseOptions => BaseOptions(
    baseUrl: baseUrl,
    headers: headers,
    connectTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 5000),
    receiveTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 5000),
    sendTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 5000),
  );

  @override
  String get baseUrl => EnvInfo.baseUrl;

  @override
  Map<String, Object> get headers => {
    'accept': 'application/json',
    'content-type': 'application/json',
  };

  /// Update headers (e.g., adding Auth Token after login)
  @override
  Map<String, dynamic>? updateHeader(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    Map<String, dynamic> header = {...data, ...headers};
    _dio.options.headers = header;
    return header;
  }

  // --- HTTP Methods ---

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> post(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? data,
  }) async {
    // handleException wraps the call to catch errors automatically
    Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> res = handleException(
      () => _dio.post(endpoint, data: data),
      endpoint: endpoint,
    );
    return res;
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> get(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> res = handleException(
      () => _dio.get(endpoint, queryParameters: queryParameters),
      endpoint: endpoint,
    );
    return res;
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> put(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? data,
  }) async {
    Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> res = handleException(
      () => _dio.put(endpoint, data: data),
      endpoint: endpoint,
    );
    return res;
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> delete(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> res = handleException(
      () => _dio.delete(endpoint, queryParameters: queryParameters),
      endpoint: endpoint,
    );
    return res;
  }

  /// Upload files (images, documents)
  Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> uploadFile(
    String endpoint,
    FormData formData,
  ) async {
    Future<Either<AppException, response.Response>> res = handleException(
      () => _dio.post(endpoint, data: formData),
      endpoint: endpoint,
    );
    return res;
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/logout_service_function.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/routes.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/configuration.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/routes/app_routes.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// LogoutService
///
/// A single Singleton class to handle the entire logout process.
/// This ensures logout logic is consistent properly everywhere.
class LogoutService {
  // Private constructor
  LogoutService._internal({
    required this.hiveService,
    required this.networkService,
    required this.userPreferences,
  });

  // The single instance of this class
  static final LogoutService _instance = LogoutService._internal(
    hiveService: GetIt.instance<HiveService>(),
    networkService: GetIt.instance<NetworkService>(),
    userPreferences: UserPreferences.instance,
  );

  // Access point for the instance
  static LogoutService get instance => _instance;

  final HiveService hiveService;
  final NetworkService? networkService;
  final UserPreferences userPreferences;

  /// Performs full logout and redirects to Login screen
  Future<void> logoutAndNavigate() async {
    await clearData();
  }

  /// Clears all user data
  /// 1. Clears local database (Hive)
  /// 2. Clears in-memory user preferences
  /// 3. Removes Auth Token from API headers
  /// 4. Navigates to Login Screen
  Future<void> clearData() async {
    final hive = hiveService;
    await hive.clear();
    userPreferences.clearPreferences();
    networkService?.updateHeader({'Authorization': ''});
    navigatorKey.currentContext?.go(RoutesName.loginPath);
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/model/either.dart <==
/// This is a functional programming concept. "Either" means the result will be
/// ONE of two things, never both.
///
/// - Left (L): Usually represents Failure / Error.
/// - Right (R): Usually represents Success / Data.
///
/// We use this for API calls so we are forced to handle both cases.
sealed class Either<L, R> {
  const Either();
  factory Either.left(L l) => Left(l);
  factory Either.right(R r) => Right(r);

  // "folds" the result: execute 'left' func if Left, 'right' func if Right
  T fold<T>(T Function(L) left, T Function(R) right) => switch (this) {
        Left(:final value) => left(value),
        Right(:final value) => right(value),
      };

  bool isLeft() => switch (this) {
        Left() => true,
        Right() => false,
      };
  bool isRight() => !isLeft();
}

/// Represents the Left side (Error)
class Left<L, R> extends Either<L, R> {
  final L _l;
  const Left(this._l);
  L get value => _l;
}

/// Represents the Right side (Success)
class Right<L, R> extends Either<L, R> {
  final R _r;
  const Right(this._r);
  R get value => _r;
}

==> lib/core/network/model/response.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'either.dart';

/// Response
///
/// A generic wrapper for any "Success" response from the API.
///
/// Contains:
/// - statusCode: 200, 201, etc.
/// - data: The actual JSON body.
class Response {
  final int statusCode;
  final String? statusMessage;
  final dynamic data;

  Response({
    required this.statusCode,
    this.statusMessage,
    this.data = const {},
  });

  @override
  String toString() {
    return 'statusCode=$statusCode\nstatusMessage=$statusMessage\n data=$data';
  }
}

extension ResponseExtension on Response {
  // Helper to convert to Either Type (Right side = Success)
  Right<AppException, Response> get toRight => Right(this);
}

==> lib/core/network/model/response/base_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

BaseResponse baseResponseFromJson(String str) => BaseResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));
String baseResponseToJson(BaseResponse data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

/// BaseResponse handles common outer structure of API responses.
class BaseResponse {
  num? status;
  String? message;

  BaseResponse({
    this.status,
    this.message,
  });

  BaseResponse.fromJson(dynamic json) {
    status = json['status'];
    message = json['message'];
  }

  BaseResponse copyWith({
    num? status,
    String? message,
  }) {
    return BaseResponse(
      status: status ?? this.status,
      message: message ?? this.message,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    final map = <String, dynamic>{};
    map['status'] = status;
    map['message'] = message;
    return map;
  }
}

==> lib/core/network/network_service.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';

import 'model/either.dart';
import 'model/response.dart';

/// NetworkService
///
/// This is an "Abstract Class" or Interface.
///
/// Why do we need this?
/// - It defines strict rules for any network service we create.
/// - It allows us to easily switch from Dio to another library (like Http) in the future without breaking the app.
/// - It makes testing easier because we can create a "FakeNetworkService".
abstract class NetworkService {
  String get baseUrl;

  Map<String, Object> get headers;

  /// Updates headers (usually for adding the Auth Token)
  void updateHeader(Map<String, dynamic> data);

  Future<Either<AppException, Response>> get(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, Response>> post(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? data,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, Response>> put(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? data,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, Response>> delete(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  });
}

==> lib/core/services/permission_service.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:permission_handler/permission_handler.dart';

/// PermissionService
///
/// Manages system permissions (Camera, Photos, etc.)
class PermissionService {
  static bool _isRequesting = false;

  /// Checks and requests Photo/Gallery permission
  static Future<bool> requestPhotoPermission() async {
    if (_isRequesting) return false;
    _isRequesting = true;
    try {
      final status = await Permission.photos.request();
      return status.isGranted;
    } catch (e) {
      ErrorLogger.log('PermissionService.requestPhotoPermission', e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      _isRequesting = false;
    }
  }

  /// Checks and requests Camera permission
  static Future<bool> requestCameraPermission() async {
    if (_isRequesting) return false;
    _isRequesting = true;
    try {
      final status = await Permission.camera.request();
      return status.isGranted;
    } catch (e) {
      ErrorLogger.log('PermissionService.requestCameraPermission', e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      _isRequesting = false;
    }
  }

  /// Opens App Settings if permission is permanently denied
  static Future<void> openSettings() async {
    try {
      await openAppSettings();
    } catch (e) {
      ErrorLogger.log('PermissionService.openSettings', e);
    }
  }
}

==> lib/core/services/url_service.dart <==
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

/// UrlService
/// 
/// A service to launch external URLs, phone calls, and emails.
class UrlService {
  /// Launches a web URL in the system browser
  static Future<void> launchWebUrl(String url) async {
    final Uri uri = Uri.parse(url);
    if (!await launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication)) {
      throw 'Could not launch $url';
    }
  }

  /// Initiates a phone call
  static Future<void> makePhoneCall(String phoneNumber) async {
    final Uri uri = Uri.parse('tel:$phoneNumber');
    if (!await launchUrl(uri)) {
      throw 'Could not call $phoneNumber';
    }
  }

  /// Opens the email app
  static Future<void> sendEmail(String email, {String? subject}) async {
    final Uri uri = Uri.parse('mailto:$email?subject=${subject ?? ''}');
    if (!await launchUrl(uri)) {
      throw 'Could not email $email';
    }
  }
}

==> lib/core/utils/configuration.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/extension/roles.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

/// UserPreferences
///
/// This class holds "In-Memory" user data using the Singleton pattern.
///
/// What does that mean?
/// - "Singleton": There is only ONE copy of this class in the entire app.
/// - "In-Memory": If you close the app completely, this data is lost (unless we save it to Hive).
///
/// We use this to quickly access user info (like Name, Role, Token) anywhere in the app
/// without reading from the database every single time.
class UserPreferences {
  // Private constructor (prevents creating new instances)
  UserPreferences._();

  // The single instance
  static final UserPreferences _instance = UserPreferences._();

  // Access the instance
  static UserPreferences get instance => _instance;

  // --- Stored Data ---
  UserData? _user;
  UserRole? _userRole;
  String? _fcmToken;
  bool notificationReadIcon = false;

  /// Setup user after login
  void setUser(UserData user) {
    _user = user;
  }

  UserData? getUser() {
    return _user;
  }

  /// Store user role (Admin vs Customer)
  void setUserRole(UserRole role) {
    _userRole = role;
  }

  UserRole? getUserRole() {
    return _userRole;
  }

  /// Store Push Notification Token (Firebase)
  String? getFCMToken() {
    return _fcmToken;
  }

  void setFCMToken(String token) {
    _fcmToken = token;
  }

  /// Manage notification badge
  void setNotificationReadIcon(bool value) {
    notificationReadIcon = value;
  }

  bool getNotificationReadIcon() {
    return notificationReadIcon;
  }

  /// Clear everything (Logout)
  void clearPreferences() {
    _user = null;
    _userRole = null;
    _fcmToken = null;
  }
}

==> lib/core/utils/error_logger.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

/// ErrorLogger
///
/// A helper class to standardize how we handle and print errors.
class ErrorLogger {
  /// Logs an error to the console with a nice visual border.
  /// Only prints in Debug mode.
  static void log(String identifier, dynamic error) {
    debugPrint('═══════════════════════════════════════');
    debugPrint('🔴 ERROR: $identifier');
    debugPrint('Details: ${error.toString()}');
    debugPrint('═══════════════════════════════════════');
  }

  /// Helps catch errors in DataSources and return them as an `AppException`.
  ///
  /// Usage:
  /// ```dart
  /// try {
  ///   // API call
  /// } catch (e) {
  ///   return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'ClassName.methodName');
  /// }
  /// ```
  static Left<AppException, T> handleException<T>(
    dynamic exception,
    String identifier, {
    String? customMessage,
  }) {
    // 1. Log to console
    log(identifier, exception);

    // 2. Return standard error object (Left)
    return Left(
      AppException(
        message: customMessage ?? AppStrings.unknownError,
        statusCode: 1,
        identifier: '${exception.toString()}\n$identifier',
      ),
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/cart/data/data_source/cart_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/add_to_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/cart_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/remove_from_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/update_cart_request.dart';

abstract class CartRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> getCart();

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> addItem(AddToCartRequest request);

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> updateQuantity(
    UpdateCartRequest request,
  );

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> removeItem(
    RemoveFromCartRequest request,
  );

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> clearCart();
}

class CartRemoteDataSourceImpl implements CartRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  CartRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> getCart() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(ApiEndpoint.cart);
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        CartResponse res = CartResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'CartRemoteDataSource.getCart');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> addItem(
    AddToCartRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.cartItems,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        CartResponse res = CartResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'CartRemoteDataSource.addItem');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> updateQuantity(
    UpdateCartRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.put(
        '${ApiEndpoint.cartItems}/${request.productId}',
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        CartResponse res = CartResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'CartRemoteDataSource.updateQuantity',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> removeItem(
    RemoveFromCartRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.delete(
        '${ApiEndpoint.cartItems}/${request.productId}',
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        CartResponse res = CartResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'CartRemoteDataSource.removeItem');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> clearCart() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.delete(ApiEndpoint.cart);
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        CartResponse res = CartResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'CartRemoteDataSource.clearCart');
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';

CartResponse cartResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    CartResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String cartResponseToJson(CartResponse data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

class CartResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final CartData? data;

  const CartResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory CartResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => CartResponse(
    status: json["status"] ?? false,
    message: json["message"] ?? "",
    data: json["data"] != null ? CartData.fromJson(json["data"]) : null,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class CartData {
  final List<CartItemModel>? items;
  final int? itemCount;
  final num? subtotal;
  final num? savings;
  final num? deliveryFee;
  final num? grandTotal;

  const CartData({
    this.items,
    this.itemCount,
    this.subtotal,
    this.savings,
    this.deliveryFee,
    this.grandTotal,
  });

  factory CartData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => CartData(
    items: json["items"] != null
        ? List<CartItemModel>.from(
            json["items"].map((x) => CartItemModel.fromJson(x)),
          )
        : [],
    itemCount: json["itemCount"] ?? 0,
    subtotal: json["subtotal"] ?? 0,
    savings: json["savings"] ?? 0,
    deliveryFee: json["deliveryFee"] ?? 0,
    grandTotal: json["grandTotal"] ?? 0,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (items != null)
      "items": List<dynamic>.from(items!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
    "itemCount": itemCount,
    "subtotal": subtotal,
    "savings": savings,
    "deliveryFee": deliveryFee,
    "grandTotal": grandTotal,
  };
}

    this.totalPrice,
    this.imageUrl,
    this.stock,

    productId: json["productId"] ?? "",
    productName: json["productName"] ?? "",
    slug: json["slug"] ?? "",
    packSize: json["packSize"] ?? "",
    unitPrice: json["unitPrice"] ?? 0,
    mrp: json["mrp"] ?? 0,
    quantity: json["quantity"] ?? 0,
    totalPrice: json["totalPrice"] ?? 0,
    imageUrl: json["imageUrl"] ?? "",
    stock: json["stock"] ?? 0,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "id": id,
    "productId": productId,
    "productName": productName,

    "packSize": packSize,
    "unitPrice": unitPrice,

    "imageUrl": imageUrl,
    "stock": stock,
  };

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/add_to_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/cart_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/remove_from_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/update_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/domain/repositories/cart_repository.dart';

class CartUseCase {
  final CartRepository _repository;

  CartUseCase(this._repository);

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> getCart() {
    return _repository.getCart();
  }

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> addItem(AddToCartRequest request) {
    return _repository.addItem(request);
  }

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> updateQuantity(
    UpdateCartRequest request,
  ) {
    return _repository.updateQuantity(request);
  }

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> removeItem(
    RemoveFromCartRequest request,
  ) {
    return _repository.removeItem(request);
  }

  Future<Either<AppException, CartResponse>> clearCart() {
    return _repository.clearCart();
  }
}

==> lib/features/cart/presentation/cubit/cart_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/add_to_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/cart_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/remove_from_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/data/models/update_cart_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/cart/domain/use_case/cart_use_case.dart';

part 'cart_state.dart';

class CartCubit extends Cubit<CartState> {
  final CartUseCase _cartUseCase;

  CartCubit(this._cartUseCase) : super(const CartState());

  Future<void> fetchCart({bool showLoading = true}) async {
    if (showLoading) {
      emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));
    }

    final result = await _cartUseCase.getCart();

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('CartCubit.fetchCart', error.message);
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message));
      },
      (response) {
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, cartData: response.data));
      },
    );
  }

  Future<bool> addToCart(String productId, {int quantity = 1}) async {
    final request = AddToCartRequest(productId: productId, quantity: quantity);
    final result = await _cartUseCase.addItem(request);

    return result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('CartCubit.addToCart', error.message);
        emit(state.copyWith(errorMessage: error.message));
        return false;
      },
      (response) {
        emit(state.copyWith(cartData: response.data));
        return true;
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> updateQuantity(String productId, int newQuantity) async {
    if (newQuantity <= 0) {
      await removeItem(productId);
      return;
    }

    emit(state.copyWith(updatingProductId: productId));

    final request = UpdateCartRequest(
      productId: productId,
      quantity: newQuantity,
    );
    final result = await _cartUseCase.updateQuantity(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('CartCubit.updateQuantity', error.message);
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            clearUpdatingProduct: true,
            errorMessage: error.message,
          ),
        );
      },
      (response) {
        emit(
          state.copyWith(clearUpdatingProduct: true, cartData: response.data),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> removeItem(String productId) async {
    emit(state.copyWith(updatingProductId: productId));

    final request = RemoveFromCartRequest(productId: productId);
    final result = await _cartUseCase.removeItem(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('CartCubit.removeItem', error.message);
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            clearUpdatingProduct: true,
            errorMessage: error.message,
          ),
        );
      },
      (response) {
        emit(
          state.copyWith(clearUpdatingProduct: true, cartData: response.data),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> clearCart() async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true));

    final result = await _cartUseCase.clearCart();

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('CartCubit.clearCart', error.message);
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message));
      },
      (response) {
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, cartData: response.data));
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/cart/presentation/cubit/cart_state.dart <==
part of 'cart_cubit.dart';

class CartState extends Equatable {
  final CartData? cartData;
  final bool isLoading;
  final String errorMessage;
  final String? updatingProductId;

  const CartState({
    this.cartData,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.updatingProductId,
  });

  CartState copyWith({
    CartData? cartData,
    bool? isLoading,
    String? errorMessage,
    String? updatingProductId,
    bool clearUpdatingProduct = false,
  }) {
    return CartState(
      cartData: cartData ?? this.cartData,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      updatingProductId: clearUpdatingProduct
          ? null
          : (updatingProductId ?? this.updatingProductId),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    cartData,
    isLoading,
    errorMessage,
    updatingProductId,
  ];
}

class CartPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CartPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CartPage> createState() => _CartPageState();
}

class _CartPageState extends State<CartPage> {
  late CartCubit _cartCubit;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cartCubit = CartCubit(injector<CartUseCase>())..fetchCart();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _cartCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _cartCubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              size: Dimens.standard_20,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          title: Text(
            AppStrings.myCart,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            BlocBuilder<CartCubit, CartState>(
              builder: (context, state) {
                final hasItems = (state.cartData?.items?.isNotEmpty ?? false);
                if (!hasItems) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                return IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(
                    Icons.delete_outline,
                    color: AppColors.colorRed,
                  ),
                  tooltip: AppStrings.clearCart,
                  onPressed: () => _showClearCartDialog(context),
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<CartCubit, CartState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state.isLoading) {
              return const Center(child: CustomLoader());
            }

            final cartData = state.cartData;
            final items = cartData?.items ?? [];

            if (items.isEmpty) {
              return _buildEmptyCart(context);
            }

            return Column(
              children: [
                // Delivery guarantee banner
                Container(
                  color: AppColors.colorGreen.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: Dimens.standard_16,
                    vertical: Dimens.standard_10,
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(
                        Icons.bolt,
                        color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                        size: Dimens.standard_20,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
                      Text(
                        AppStrings.deliveryInMins,
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12.copyWith(
                          color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                Expanded(
                  child: ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
                    children: [
                      // Cart Items Card
                      Container(
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.cardBackground,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                            Dimens.standard_12,
                          ),
                        ),
                        child: ListView.separated(
                          shrinkWrap: true,
                          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                          itemCount: items.length,
                          separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(
                            height: Dimens.standard_1,
                            indent: Dimens.standard_16,
                            endIndent: Dimens.standard_16,
                            color: AppColors.dividerColor,
                          ),
                          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                            final item = items[index];
                            return _buildCartItemTile(item, state);
                          },
                        ),
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),

                      // Bill Details Card
                      _buildBillDetails(cartData!),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_24),
                    ],

                _buildBottomCheckoutBar(context, cartData),

          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCartItemTile(CartItemModel item, CartState state) {
    final isUpdating = state.updatingProductId == item.productId;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          // Thumbnail

            child: CustomNetworkImage(
              imageUrl: item.imageUrl ?? '',
              width: Dimens.standard_56,
              height: Dimens.standard_56,
              fit: BoxFit.cover,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_12),

          // Name & Pack size
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  item.productName ?? '',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                  maxLines: 2,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_4),
                Text(
                  item.packSize ?? '',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_6),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '₹${item.unitPrice ?? 0}',
                      style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                        color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (item.mrp != null &&
                        item.unitPrice != null &&
                        item.mrp! > item.unitPrice!) ...[
                      const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_6),
                      Text(
                        '₹${item.mrp}',
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                          decoration: TextDecoration.lineThrough,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          // Stepper Quantity Control
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.colorLightGrey),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_8),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.remove, size: Dimens.standard_16),
                  onPressed: isUpdating
                      ? null
                      : () {
                          final currentQty = item.quantity ?? 1;
                          _cartCubit.updateQuantity(
                            item.productId ?? '',
                            currentQty - 1,
                          );
                        },
                ),
                if (isUpdating)
                  const SizedBox(
                    width: Dimens.standard_16,
                    height: Dimens.standard_16,
                    child: CustomLoader(
                      size: Dimens.standard_16,
                      strokeWidth: Dimens.standard_2,
                      isCentered: false,
                    ),
                  )
                else
                  Text(
                    '${item.quantity ?? 1}',
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14,
                  ),
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.add, size: Dimens.standard_16),
                  onPressed: isUpdating
                      ? null
                      : () {
                          final currentQty = item.quantity ?? 1;
                          _cartCubit.updateQuantity(
                            item.productId ?? '',
                            currentQty + 1,
                          );
                        },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

              ? AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                )
              : AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

==> lib/features/checkout/data/data_source/checkout_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/address_list_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/checkout_summary_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/checkout_summary_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/create_address_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/create_order_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/order_response.dart';

abstract class CheckoutRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, AddressListResponse>> getAddresses();

  Future<Either<AppException, AddressListResponse>> createAddress(
    CreateAddressRequest request,
  );

  Future<Either<AppException, CheckoutSummaryResponse>> getCheckoutSummary(
    CheckoutSummaryRequest request,
  );

  Future<Either<AppException, OrderResponse>> placeOrder(
    CreateOrderRequest request,
  );
}

class CheckoutRemoteDataSourceImpl implements CheckoutRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  CheckoutRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, AddressListResponse>> getAddresses() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(ApiEndpoint.addresses);
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = AddressListResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'CheckoutRemoteDataSource.getAddresses',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, AddressListResponse>> createAddress(
    CreateAddressRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.addresses,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) async {
        // Re-fetch addresses list after creation
        return getAddresses();
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'CheckoutRemoteDataSource.createAddress',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CheckoutSummaryResponse>> getCheckoutSummary(
    CheckoutSummaryRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.checkoutSummary,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = CheckoutSummaryResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'CheckoutRemoteDataSource.getCheckoutSummary',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderResponse>> placeOrder(
    CreateOrderRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.orders,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = OrderResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'CheckoutRemoteDataSource.placeOrder',
      );
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/checkout/data/models/address_list_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/address_model.dart';

AddressListResponse addressListResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    AddressListResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String addressListResponseToJson(AddressListResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class AddressListResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final AddressListData? data;

  const AddressListResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory AddressListResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      AddressListResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null
            ? AddressListData.fromJson(json["data"])
            : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class AddressListData {
  final List<AddressModel>? items;

  const AddressListData({this.items});

  factory AddressListData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      AddressListData(
        items: json["items"] != null
            ? List<AddressModel>.from(
                json["items"].map((x) => AddressModel.fromJson(x)),
              )
            : [],
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (items != null)
      "items": List<dynamic>.from(items!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
  };
}

    CreateOrderRequest request,

  }
