==> lib/features/checkout/presentation/cubit/checkout_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/address_model.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/checkout_summary_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/checkout_summary_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/create_address_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/create_order_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/order_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/domain/use_case/checkout_use_case.dart';

part 'checkout_state.dart';

class CheckoutCubit extends Cubit<CheckoutState> {
  final CheckoutUseCase _checkoutUseCase;

  CheckoutCubit(this._checkoutUseCase) : super(const CheckoutState());

  Future<void> init() async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final addressResult = await _checkoutUseCase.getAddresses();

    addressResult.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (addressResponse) async {
        final addresses = addressResponse.data?.items ?? [];
        final defaultAddress =
            addresses.where((a) => a.isDefault == true).firstOrNull ??
            addresses.firstOrNull;

        emit(
          state.copyWith(addresses: addresses, selectedAddress: defaultAddress),
        );

        if (defaultAddress != null && defaultAddress.id != null) {
          await _fetchSummary(defaultAddress.id!);
        } else {
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false));
        }
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> selectAddress(AddressModel address) async {
    if (state.selectedAddress?.id == address.id) return;
    emit(state.copyWith(selectedAddress: address, isLoading: true));
    if (address.id != null) {
      await _fetchSummary(address.id!);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _fetchSummary(String addressId) async {
    final result = await _checkoutUseCase.getCheckoutSummary(
      CheckoutSummaryRequest(addressId: addressId),
    );

    result.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (summaryResponse) {
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, summary: summaryResponse.data));
      },
    );
  }

  void selectPaymentMethod(String method) {
    emit(state.copyWith(paymentMethod: method));
  }

  void updateDeliveryInstructions(String text) {
    emit(state.copyWith(deliveryInstructions: text));
  }

  Future<void> addNewAddress(CreateAddressRequest request) async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isAddingAddress: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final result = await _checkoutUseCase.createAddress(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) => emit(
        state.copyWith(isAddingAddress: false, errorMessage: error.message),
      ),
      (response) async {
        final addresses = response.data?.items ?? [];
        final newAddress = addresses.lastOrNull ?? addresses.firstOrNull;

        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isAddingAddress: false,
            addresses: addresses,
            selectedAddress: newAddress,
          ),
        );

        if (newAddress != null && newAddress.id != null) {
          await _fetchSummary(newAddress.id!);
        }
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> placeOrder() async {
    if (state.selectedAddress == null || state.selectedAddress?.id == null) {
      emit(state.copyWith(errorMessage: 'Please select a delivery address'));
      return;
    }

    emit(state.copyWith(isPlacingOrder: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final request = CreateOrderRequest(
      addressId: state.selectedAddress!.id,
      paymentMethod: state.paymentMethod,
      deliveryInstructions: state.deliveryInstructions,
    );

    final result = await _checkoutUseCase.placeOrder(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) => emit(
        state.copyWith(isPlacingOrder: false, errorMessage: error.message),
      ),
      (orderResponse) {
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isPlacingOrder: false,
            orderSuccess: true,
            placedOrder: orderResponse.data,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/checkout/presentation/cubit/checkout_state.dart <==
part of 'checkout_cubit.dart';

class CheckoutState extends Equatable {
  final List<AddressModel> addresses;
  final AddressModel? selectedAddress;
  final CheckoutSummaryData? summary;
  final String paymentMethod;
  final String deliveryInstructions;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool isPlacingOrder;
  final bool isAddingAddress;
  final OrderData? placedOrder;
  final String errorMessage;
  final bool orderSuccess;

  const CheckoutState({
    this.addresses = const [],
    this.selectedAddress,
    this.summary,
    this.paymentMethod = 'COD',
    this.deliveryInstructions = '',
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.isPlacingOrder = false,
    this.isAddingAddress = false,
    this.placedOrder,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.orderSuccess = false,
  });

  CheckoutState copyWith({
    List<AddressModel>? addresses,
    AddressModel? selectedAddress,
    CheckoutSummaryData? summary,
    String? paymentMethod,
    String? deliveryInstructions,
    bool? isLoading,
    bool? isPlacingOrder,
    bool? isAddingAddress,
    OrderData? placedOrder,
    String? errorMessage,
    bool? orderSuccess,
  }) {
    return CheckoutState(
      addresses: addresses ?? this.addresses,
      selectedAddress: selectedAddress ?? this.selectedAddress,
      summary: summary ?? this.summary,
      paymentMethod: paymentMethod ?? this.paymentMethod,
      deliveryInstructions: deliveryInstructions ?? this.deliveryInstructions,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      isPlacingOrder: isPlacingOrder ?? this.isPlacingOrder,
      isAddingAddress: isAddingAddress ?? this.isAddingAddress,
      placedOrder: placedOrder ?? this.placedOrder,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      orderSuccess: orderSuccess ?? this.orderSuccess,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    addresses,
    selectedAddress,
    summary,
    paymentMethod,
    deliveryInstructions,
    isLoading,
    isPlacingOrder,
    isAddingAddress,
    placedOrder,
    errorMessage,
    orderSuccess,
  ];
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/routes.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/dependency_injection/injector.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/data/models/create_address_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/domain/use_case/checkout_use_case.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/checkout/presentation/cubit/checkout_cubit.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/config/dimens.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/theme/text_styles.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/widgets/custom_loader.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/widgets/primary_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

class CheckoutPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CheckoutPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CheckoutPage> createState() => _CheckoutPageState();
}

class _CheckoutPageState extends State<CheckoutPage> {
  late CheckoutCubit _checkoutCubit;
  final TextEditingController _instructionsController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkoutCubit = CheckoutCubit(injector<CheckoutUseCase>())..init();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _instructionsController.dispose();
    _checkoutCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _checkoutCubit,
      child: BlocConsumer<CheckoutCubit, CheckoutState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          if (state.errorMessage.isNotEmpty) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(state.errorMessage),
                backgroundColor: AppColors.colorRed,
              ),
            );
          }
          if (state.orderSuccess && state.placedOrder != null) {
            context.go(RoutesName.orderSuccess, extra: state.placedOrder);
          }
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          return Scaffold(
            backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
            appBar: AppBar(
              backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
              elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
              leading: IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(
                  Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  size: Dimens.standard_20,
                ),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
              ),
              title: Text(
                AppStrings.checkout,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            body: state.isLoading
                ? const Center(child: CustomLoader())
                : ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
                    children: [
                      // Delivery Address Section
                      _buildAddressSection(context, state),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),

                      // Delivery Instructions
                      _buildInstructionsSection(context),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),

                      // Payment Method Section
                      _buildPaymentSection(state),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),

                      // Bill Summary
                      if (state.summary != null) ...[
                        _buildBillSummary(state),
                        const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_24),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
            bottomNavigationBar: _buildBottomBar(context, state),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAddressSection(BuildContext context, CheckoutState state) {
    final selected = state.selectedAddress;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.cardBackground,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  const Icon(
                    Icons.location_on_outlined,
                    color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                    size: Dimens.standard_20,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
                  Text(
                    AppStrings.deliveryAddress,
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                      color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              TextButton(
                onPressed: () => _showAddressPicker(context, state),
                child: Text(
                  selected == null ? AppStrings.addAddress : AppStrings.change,
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
          if (selected != null) ...[
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: Dimens.standard_8,
                vertical: Dimens.standard_4,
              ),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.colorGreen.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_4),
              ),
              child: Text(
                (selected.type ?? AppStrings.home).toUpperCase(),
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
            Text(
              '${selected.addressLine1 ?? ''}, ${selected.addressLine2 ?? ''}',
              style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              ),
            ),
            if (selected.landmark != null && selected.landmark!.isNotEmpty)
              Text(
                '${AppStrings.landmark}: ${selected.landmark}',
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
            Text(
              '${selected.city ?? ''}, ${selected.state ?? ''} - ${selected.pincode ?? ''}',
              style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
            if (selected.phone != null && selected.phone!.isNotEmpty)
              Text(
                '${AppStrings.phone}: ${selected.phone}',
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
          ] else ...[
            Text(
              AppStrings.noDeliveryAddressSaved,
              style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInstructionsSection(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.cardBackground,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.edit_note,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                size: Dimens.standard_20,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
              Text(
                AppStrings.deliveryInstructions,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
          TextField(
            controller: _instructionsController,
            onChanged: (val) => _checkoutCubit.updateDeliveryInstructions(val),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: AppStrings.instructionsHint,
              hintStyle: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
              contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: Dimens.standard_12,
                vertical: Dimens.standard_10,
              ),
              border: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_8),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.inputBorder),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPaymentSection(CheckoutState state) {
    final options = [
      {
        'key': 'COD',
        'title': AppStrings.cashOnDelivery,
        'subtitle': AppStrings.cashOnDeliverySub,
      },
      {
        'key': 'UPI',
        'title': AppStrings.upiInstant,
        'subtitle': AppStrings.upiInstantSub,
      },
      {
        'key': 'CARD',
        'title': AppStrings.cardPayment,
        'subtitle': AppStrings.cardPaymentSub,
      },
    ];

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.cardBackground,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.payment,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                size: Dimens.standard_20,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
              Text(
                AppStrings.paymentMethod,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
          ...options.map((opt) {
            final isSelected = state.paymentMethod == opt['key'];
            return InkWell(
              onTap: () => _checkoutCubit.selectPaymentMethod(opt['key']!),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  vertical: Dimens.standard_8,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      isSelected
                          ? Icons.radio_button_checked
                          : Icons.radio_button_off,
                      color: isSelected
                          ? AppColors.colorGreen
                          : AppColors.colorGrey,
                      size: Dimens.standard_20,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            opt['title']!,
                            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            opt['subtitle']!,
                            style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBillSummary(CheckoutState state) {
    final summary = state.summary!;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.cardBackground,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            AppStrings.billSummary,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
          _buildRow(
            '${AppStrings.itemTotal} (${summary.itemCount ?? 0})',
            '₹${summary.subtotal ?? 0}',
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
          _buildRow(
            AppStrings.deliveryFee,
            (summary.deliveryFee == null || summary.deliveryFee == 0)
                ? AppStrings.free
                : '₹${summary.deliveryFee}',
            valueColor:
                (summary.deliveryFee == null || summary.deliveryFee == 0)
                ? AppColors.colorGreen
                : null,
          ),
          if (summary.discount != null && summary.discount! > 0) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
            _buildRow(
              AppStrings.totalSavings,
              '- ₹${summary.discount}',
              valueColor: AppColors.colorGreen,
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
          _buildRow(AppStrings.taxesCharges, '₹${summary.taxes ?? 0}'),
          const Divider(
            height: Dimens.standard_24,
            color: AppColors.dividerColor,
          ),
          _buildRow(
            AppStrings.toPay,
            '₹${summary.grandTotal ?? 0}',
            isBold: true,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRow(
    String label,
    String value, {
    bool isBold = false,
    Color? valueColor,
  }) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: isBold
              ? AppTextStyles.openSansBold14
              : AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
        ),
        Text(
          value,
          style: isBold
              ? AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                )
              : AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBottomBar(BuildContext context, CheckoutState state) {
    final total = state.summary?.grandTotal ?? 0;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.colorBlack.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
            blurRadius: Dimens.standard_10,
            offset: const Offset(Dimens.standard_0, -Dimens.standard_4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: SafeArea(
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Text(
                  AppStrings.totalToPay,
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  '₹$total',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold20.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_24),
            Expanded(
              child: PrimaryButton(
                text: AppStrings.placeOrder,
                isLoading: state.isPlacingOrder,
                suffixIcon: const Icon(
                  Icons.arrow_forward,
                  size: Dimens.standard_18,
                  color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                ),
                onPressed: state.isPlacingOrder
                    ? null
                    : () => _checkoutCubit.placeOrder(),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddressPicker(BuildContext context, CheckoutState state) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(
          top: Radius.circular(Dimens.standard_16),
        ),
      ),
      builder: (bottomSheetContext) {
        return Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
          constraints: BoxConstraints(
            maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(bottomSheetContext).size.height * 0.7,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    AppStrings.selectDeliveryAddress,
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                      color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(bottomSheetContext).pop(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const Divider(color: AppColors.dividerColor),
              Expanded(
                child: ListView.separated(
                  itemCount: state.addresses.length,
                  separatorBuilder: (_, _) =>
                      const Divider(color: AppColors.dividerColor),
                  itemBuilder: (_, index) {
                    final addr = state.addresses[index];
                    final isSelected = state.selectedAddress?.id == addr.id;
                    return ListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      leading: Icon(
                        isSelected
                            ? Icons.check_circle
                            : Icons.radio_button_off,
                        color: isSelected
                            ? AppColors.colorGreen
                            : AppColors.colorGrey,
                      ),
                      title: Text(
                        '${addr.type?.toUpperCase() ?? AppStrings.home.toUpperCase()} - ${addr.addressLine1 ?? ''}',
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14,
                      ),
                      subtitle: Text(
                        '${addr.city ?? ''}, ${addr.state ?? ''} - ${addr.pincode ?? ''}',
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12,
                      ),
                      onTap: () {
                        _checkoutCubit.selectAddress(addr);
                        Navigator.of(bottomSheetContext).pop();
                      },
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
              PrimaryButton(
                text: AppStrings.addNewAddress,
                isOutlined: true,
                prefixIcon: const Icon(
                  Icons.add,
                  color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                  size: Dimens.standard_18,
                ),
                onPressed: () {
                  Navigator.of(bottomSheetContext).pop();
                  _showAddAddressDialog(context);
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showAddAddressDialog(BuildContext context) {
    final line1Ctrl = TextEditingController();
    final line2Ctrl = TextEditingController();
    final cityCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final stateCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final pinCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final phoneCtrl = TextEditingController();
    String addressType = 'Home';

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (dialogCtx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          title: Text(
            AppStrings.newAddress,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16,
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  initialValue: addressType,
                  items: [
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: 'Home',
                      child: Text(AppStrings.home),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: 'Work',
                      child: Text(AppStrings.work),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: 'Other',
                      child: Text(AppStrings.other),
                    ),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (v) =>
                      setDialogState(() => addressType = v ?? 'Home'),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: AppStrings.type),
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: line1Ctrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: AppStrings.houseStreet,
                  ),
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: line2Ctrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: AppStrings.areaColony),
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: cityCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: AppStrings.city),
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: stateCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: AppStrings.state),
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: pinCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: AppStrings.pinCode),
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                ),
                TextField(
                  controller: phoneCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: AppStrings.phoneNumber,
                  ),
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogCtx).pop(),
              child: Text(
                AppStrings.cancel,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            PrimaryButton(
              width: Dimens.standard_140,
              height: Dimens.standard_40,
              borderRadius: Dimens.standard_8,
              text: AppStrings.saveAddress,
              textStyle: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14,
              onPressed: () {
                if (line1Ctrl.text.trim().isEmpty ||
                    pinCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) {
                  return;
                }
                Navigator.of(dialogCtx).pop();
                _checkoutCubit.addNewAddress(
                  CreateAddressRequest(
                    type: addressType,
                    addressLine1: line1Ctrl.text.trim(),
                    addressLine2: line2Ctrl.text.trim(),
                    city: cityCtrl.text.trim(),
                    state: stateCtrl.text.trim(),
                    pincode: pinCtrl.text.trim(),
                    phone: phoneCtrl.text.trim(),
                    isDefault: true,
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class OrderSuccessPage extends StatelessWidget {

==> lib/features/grocery/data/data_source/grocery_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/category_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_detail_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_detail_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_response.dart';

abstract class GroceryRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, ProductResponse>> getGroceryItems({
    ProductRequest? request,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, CategoryResponse>> getCategories();

  Future<Either<AppException, ProductDetailResponse>> getProductDetail(
    ProductDetailRequest request,
  );
}

class GroceryRemoteDataSourceImpl implements GroceryRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  GroceryRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProductResponse>> getGroceryItems({
    ProductRequest? request,
  }) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(
        ApiEndpoint.products,
        queryParameters: request?.toQueryParams(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold(
        (exception) {
          return Left(exception);
        },
        (response) {
          ProductResponse res = ProductResponse.fromJson(response.data);
          return Right(res);
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'GroceryRemoteDataSource.getGroceryItems',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CategoryResponse>> getCategories() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(ApiEndpoint.categories);
      return eitherType.fold(
        (exception) {
          return Left(exception);
        },
        (response) {
          CategoryResponse res = CategoryResponse.fromJson(response.data);
          return Right(res);
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'GroceryRemoteDataSource.getCategories',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProductDetailResponse>> getProductDetail(
    ProductDetailRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(
        '${ApiEndpoint.products}/${request.id}',
      );
      return eitherType.fold(
        (exception) {
          return Left(exception);
        },
        (response) {
          ProductDetailResponse res = ProductDetailResponse.fromJson(
            response.data,
          );
          return Right(res);
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'GroceryRemoteDataSource.getProductDetail',
      );
    }
  }
}

    this.displayOrder,

    slug: json["slug"] ?? "",

    bannerUrl: json["bannerUrl"] ?? "",

        : [],
  );

    "id": id,
    "name": name,
    "slug": slug,

    "bannerUrl": bannerUrl,

==> lib/features/grocery/data/models/product_request.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

ProductRequest productRequestFromJson(String str) =>
    ProductRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String productRequestToJson(ProductRequest data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

class ProductRequest {
  final int? page;
  final int? limit;
  final String? categoryId;
  final String? subcategoryId;
  final String? search;
  final String? sortBy;

  const ProductRequest({
    this.page,
    this.limit,
    this.categoryId,
    this.subcategoryId,
    this.search,
    this.sortBy,
  });

  factory ProductRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ProductRequest(
    page: json["page"],
    limit: json["limit"],
    categoryId: json["categoryId"] ?? "",
    subcategoryId: json["subcategoryId"] ?? "",
    search: json["search"] ?? "",
    sortBy: json["sortBy"] ?? "",
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (page != null) "page": page,
    if (limit != null) "limit": limit,
    if (categoryId != null && categoryId!.isNotEmpty) "categoryId": categoryId,
    if (subcategoryId != null && subcategoryId!.isNotEmpty)
      "subcategoryId": subcategoryId,
    if (search != null && search!.isNotEmpty) "search": search,
    if (sortBy != null && sortBy!.isNotEmpty) "sortBy": sortBy,
  };

  Map<String, dynamic> toQueryParams() {
    final Map<String, dynamic> params = {};
    if (page != null) {
      params["page"] = page;
    }
    if (limit != null) {
      params["limit"] = limit;
    }
    if (categoryId != null && categoryId!.isNotEmpty) {
      params["categoryId"] = categoryId;
    }
    if (subcategoryId != null && subcategoryId!.isNotEmpty) {
      params["subcategoryId"] = subcategoryId;
    }
    if (search != null && search!.isNotEmpty) {
      params["search"] = search;
    }
    if (sortBy != null && sortBy!.isNotEmpty) {
      params["sortBy"] = sortBy;
    }
    return params;
  }
}

class GroceryRepositoryImpl implements GroceryRepository {
  final GroceryRemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  GroceryRepositoryImpl(this.remoteDataSource);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProductResponse>> getGroceryItems({
    ProductRequest? request,
  }) {
    return remoteDataSource.getGroceryItems(request: request);
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, CategoryResponse>> getCategories() {
    return remoteDataSource.getCategories();
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProductDetailResponse>> getProductDetail(
    ProductDetailRequest request,
  ) {
    return remoteDataSource.getProductDetail(request);
  }

==> lib/features/grocery/presentation/cubit/grocery_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/constant.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/domain/use_case/get_grocery_items_use_case.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_model.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/category_model.dart';

part 'grocery_state.dart';

class GroceryCubit extends Cubit<GroceryState> {
  final GetGroceryItemsUseCase _getGroceryItemsUseCase;

  GroceryCubit(this._getGroceryItemsUseCase) : super(const GroceryState());

  Future<void> init() async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final categoriesResult = await _getGroceryItemsUseCase.getCategories();
    final itemsResult = await _getGroceryItemsUseCase.execute(
      request: const ProductRequest(page: 1, limit: pageLimit),
    );

    categoriesResult.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (categoryResponse) {
        final allCategories = categoryResponse.data?.items ?? [];

        itemsResult.fold(
          (error) => emit(
            state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message),
          ),
          (productResponse) {
            final items = productResponse.data?.items ?? [];
            final totalPages = productResponse.data?.pageMeta?.totalPages ?? 1;

            emit(
              state.copyWith(
                isLoading: false,
                categories: allCategories,
                allItems: items,
                items: items,
                selectedCategory: 'All',
                selectedCategoryId: null,
                currentPage: 1,
                totalPages: totalPages,
                hasReachedMax: 1 >= totalPages,
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> selectCategory(String categoryName) async {
    if (state.selectedCategory == categoryName && !state.isLoading) return;

    String? targetCategoryId;
    if (categoryName != 'All') {
      final category = state.categories
          .where((e) => e.name == categoryName)
          .firstOrNull;
      targetCategoryId = category?.id;
    }

    emit(
      state.copyWith(
        isLoading: true,
        selectedCategory: categoryName,
        selectedCategoryId: targetCategoryId,
        items: [],
        currentPage: 1,
        hasReachedMax: false,
        errorMessage: '',
      ),
    );

    final result = await _getGroceryItemsUseCase.execute(
      request: ProductRequest(
        page: 1,
        limit: pageLimit,
        categoryId: targetCategoryId,
      ),
    );

    result.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (productResponse) {
        final items = productResponse.data?.items ?? [];
        final totalPages = productResponse.data?.pageMeta?.totalPages ?? 1;

        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoading: false,
            items: items,
            allItems: items,
            currentPage: 1,
            totalPages: totalPages,
            hasReachedMax: 1 >= totalPages,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> loadMore() async {
    if (state.isLoading || state.isLoadingMore || state.hasReachedMax) return;

    emit(state.copyWith(isLoadingMore: true));

    final nextPage = state.currentPage + 1;
    final result = await _getGroceryItemsUseCase.execute(
      request: ProductRequest(
        page: nextPage,
        limit: pageLimit,
        categoryId: state.selectedCategoryId,
      ),
    );

    result.fold(
      (error) => emit(
        state.copyWith(isLoadingMore: false, errorMessage: error.message),
      ),
      (productResponse) {
        final newItems = productResponse.data?.items ?? [];
        final totalPages =
            productResponse.data?.pageMeta?.totalPages ?? state.totalPages;
        final updatedItems = [...state.items, ...newItems];
        final hasReachedMax = nextPage >= totalPages || newItems.isEmpty;

        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoadingMore: false,
            items: updatedItems,
            allItems: updatedItems,
            currentPage: nextPage,
            totalPages: totalPages,
            hasReachedMax: hasReachedMax,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/grocery/presentation/cubit/grocery_state.dart <==
part of 'grocery_cubit.dart';

class GroceryState extends Equatable {
  final List<ProductModel> allItems;
  final List<ProductModel> items;
  final List<CategoryModel> categories;
  final String selectedCategory;
  final String? selectedCategoryId;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool isLoadingMore;
  final int currentPage;
  final int totalPages;
  final bool hasReachedMax;
  final String errorMessage;

  const GroceryState({
    this.allItems = const [],
    this.items = const [],
    this.categories = const [],
    this.selectedCategory = 'All',
    this.selectedCategoryId,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.isLoadingMore = false,
    this.currentPage = 1,
    this.totalPages = 1,
    this.hasReachedMax = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
  });

  GroceryState copyWith({
    List<ProductModel>? allItems,
    List<ProductModel>? items,
    List<CategoryModel>? categories,
    String? selectedCategory,
    String? selectedCategoryId,
    bool? isLoading,
    bool? isLoadingMore,
    int? currentPage,
    int? totalPages,
    bool? hasReachedMax,
    String? errorMessage,
  }) {
    return GroceryState(
      allItems: allItems ?? this.allItems,
      items: items ?? this.items,
      categories: categories ?? this.categories,
      selectedCategory: selectedCategory ?? this.selectedCategory,
      selectedCategoryId: selectedCategoryId ?? this.selectedCategoryId,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      isLoadingMore: isLoadingMore ?? this.isLoadingMore,
      currentPage: currentPage ?? this.currentPage,
      totalPages: totalPages ?? this.totalPages,
      hasReachedMax: hasReachedMax ?? this.hasReachedMax,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    allItems,
    items,
    categories,
    selectedCategory,
    selectedCategoryId,
    isLoading,
    isLoadingMore,
    currentPage,
    totalPages,
    hasReachedMax,
    errorMessage,
  ];
}

==> lib/features/grocery/presentation/cubit/product_detail_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_detail_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_detail_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/domain/use_case/get_product_detail_use_case.dart';

part 'product_detail_state.dart';

class ProductDetailCubit extends Cubit<ProductDetailState> {
  final GetProductDetailUseCase _getProductDetailUseCase;

  ProductDetailCubit(this._getProductDetailUseCase)
    : super(const ProductDetailState());

  Future<void> fetchProductDetail(ProductDetailRequest request) async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final result = await _getProductDetailUseCase.execute(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('ProductDetailCubit.fetchProductDetail', error.message);
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message));
      },
      (response) {
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoading: false,
            product: response.data,
            selectedImageIndex: 0,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void selectImage(int index) {
    emit(state.copyWith(selectedImageIndex: index));
  }

  void incrementQuantity() {
    emit(state.copyWith(quantity: state.quantity + 1));
  }

  void decrementQuantity() {
    if (state.quantity > 1) {
      emit(state.copyWith(quantity: state.quantity - 1));
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/grocery/presentation/cubit/product_detail_state.dart <==
part of 'product_detail_cubit.dart';

class ProductDetailState extends Equatable {
  final ProductDetailModel? product;
  final bool isLoading;
  final String errorMessage;
  final int selectedImageIndex;
  final int quantity;

  const ProductDetailState({
    this.product,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.selectedImageIndex = 0,
    this.quantity = 1,
  });

  ProductDetailState copyWith({
    ProductDetailModel? product,
    bool? isLoading,
    String? errorMessage,
    int? selectedImageIndex,
    int? quantity,
  }) {
    return ProductDetailState(
      product: product ?? this.product,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      selectedImageIndex: selectedImageIndex ?? this.selectedImageIndex,
      quantity: quantity ?? this.quantity,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    product,
    isLoading,
    errorMessage,
    selectedImageIndex,
    quantity,
  ];
}

class GroceryHomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const GroceryHomePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<GroceryHomePage> createState() => _GroceryHomePageState();
}

class _GroceryHomePageState extends State<GroceryHomePage> {
  late GroceryCubit _groceryCubit;
  late WishlistCubit _wishlistCubit;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _groceryCubit = GroceryCubit(injector<GetGroceryItemsUseCase>())..init();
    _wishlistCubit = WishlistCubit(injector<WishlistUseCase>())
      ..fetchWishlist();
    _scrollController.addListener(_onScroll);
  }

  void _onScroll() {
    if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
    final maxScroll = _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
    final currentScroll = _scrollController.position.pixels;
    if (currentScroll >= maxScroll - 200) {
      _groceryCubit.loadMore();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.removeListener(_onScroll);
    _scrollController.dispose();
    _groceryCubit.close();
    _wishlistCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => _groceryCubit),
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => _wishlistCubit),
      ],
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
        drawer: const AppDrawer(),
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
          leading: Builder(
            builder: (context) => IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.menu_rounded,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                size: Dimens.standard_24,
              ),
              tooltip: 'Menu',
              onPressed: () => Scaffold.of(context).openDrawer(),
            ),
          ),
          title: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                EnvInfo.appName,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                AppStrings.deliveryHeader,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.favorite_outline,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              ),
              tooltip: AppStrings.myWishlist,
              onPressed: () => context.push(RoutesName.wishlist),
            ),
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.shopping_cart_outlined,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              ),
              tooltip: AppStrings.myCart,
              onPressed: () => context.push(RoutesName.cart),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<GroceryCubit, GroceryState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            return Column(
              children: [
                _buildCategoryList(state),
                Expanded(
                  child: Skeletonizer(
                    enabled: state.isLoading,
                    child: _buildProductGrid(state),
                  ),
                ),
                if (state.isLoadingMore)
                  const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Dimens.standard_12),
                    child: Center(
                      child: CustomLoader(
                        size: Dimens.standard_24,
                        strokeWidth: Dimens.standard_2_5,
                        color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                        isCentered: false,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCategoryList(GroceryState state) {
    final categories = ['All', ...state.categories.map((e) => e.name ?? '')];
    return Container(
      height: Dimens.standard_50,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Dimens.standard_12),
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: Dimens.standard_16),
        itemCount: state.isLoading ? 5 : categories.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          if (state.isLoading) {
            return Container(
              width: Dimens.standard_80,
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: Dimens.standard_8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.colorLightGrey,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_20),
              ),
            );
          }
          final category = categories[index];
          final isSelected = state.selectedCategory == category;
          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () {
              if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
                _scrollController.jumpTo(0);
              }
              _groceryCubit.selectCategory(category);
            },
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: Dimens.standard_20,
                vertical: Dimens.standard_8,
              ),
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: Dimens.standard_8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isSelected
                    ? AppColors.colorGreen
                    : AppColors.cardBackground,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_20),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: isSelected
                      ? AppColors.colorTransparent
                      : AppColors.inputBorder,
                ),
              ),
              child: Center(
                child: Text(
                  category.toUpperCase(),
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12.copyWith(
                    color: isSelected
                        ? AppColors.colorWhite
                        : AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProductGrid(GroceryState state) {
    if (!state.isLoading && state.items.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Text(
          AppStrings.noProductsInCategory,
          style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
            color: AppColors.textSecondary,
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    final items = state.isLoading ? _dummyItems : state.items;
    return GridView.builder(
      controller: _scrollController,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
        crossAxisCount: 2,
        childAspectRatio: 0.7,
        crossAxisSpacing: Dimens.standard_16,
        mainAxisSpacing: Dimens.standard_16,
      ),
      itemCount: items.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final item = items[index];
        return _buildProductCard(item);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProductCard(ProductModel item) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () {
        if (item.id != null && item.id!.isNotEmpty) {
          context.push(RoutesName.productDetail, extra: item.id);
        }
      },
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppColors.cardBackground,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: AppColors.colorBlack.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
              blurRadius: Dimens.standard_10,
              offset: const Offset(Dimens.standard_0, Dimens.standard_5),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                      top: Radius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
                    ),
                    child: CustomNetworkImage(
                      imageUrl: item.imageUrl ?? '',
                      width: double.infinity,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    top: Dimens.standard_6,
                    right: Dimens.standard_6,
                    child: BlocBuilder<WishlistCubit, WishlistState>(
                      builder: (context, wishlistState) {
                        final isFavorite = wishlistState.items.any(
                          (w) => w.id == item.id,
                        );
                        return CircleAvatar(
                          radius: Dimens.standard_16,
                          backgroundColor: AppColors.colorWhite.withValues(
                            alpha: 0.9,
                          ),
                          child: IconButton(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                            icon: Icon(
                              isFavorite
                                  ? Icons.favorite
                                  : Icons.favorite_border,
                              color: isFavorite
                                  ? AppColors.colorRed
                                  : AppColors.textSecondary,
                              size: Dimens.standard_18,
                            ),
                            onPressed: () =>
                                _wishlistCubit.toggleWishlist(item),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_12),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    item.name ?? '',
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14,
                    maxLines: 2,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_4),
                  Text(
                    '₹${item.discountPrice ?? item.mrp ?? 0}',
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                      color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
                  PrimaryButton(
                    text: AppStrings.add,
                    isOutlined: true,
                    height: Dimens.standard_36,
                    borderRadius: Dimens.standard_8,
                    textStyle: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12,
                    onPressed: () {
                      if (item.id != null && item.id!.isNotEmpty) {
                        injector<CartUseCase>().addItem(
                          AddToCartRequest(productId: item.id!, quantity: 1),
                        );
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          SnackBar(
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.colorGreen,
                            content: Text('${item.name} ${AppStrings.add}!'),
                            duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
                            action: SnackBarAction(
                              label: AppStrings.viewCart,
                              textColor: AppColors.colorWhite,
                              onPressed: () {
                                if (context.mounted) {
                                  context.push(RoutesName.cart);
                                }
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  final List<ProductModel> _dummyItems = List.generate(
    6,
    (index) => const ProductModel(
      id: '',
      name: 'Loading Item Name',
      discountPrice: 0,
      mrp: 0,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
  );
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';

class ProductDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String productId;

class _ProductDetailPageState extends State<ProductDetailPage> {
  late ProductDetailCubit _cubit;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cubit = ProductDetailCubit(injector<GetProductDetailUseCase>())
      ..fetchProductDetail(ProductDetailRequest(id: widget.productId));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _cubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _cubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              size: Dimens.standard_20,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.shopping_cart_outlined,

            ),
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.share_outlined,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              ),
              onPressed: () {
                final product = _cubit.state.product;
                if (product != null) {
                  ShareService.shareText(
                    'Check out ${product.name} on ${AppStrings.appName}!',
                  );
                }
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<ProductDetailCubit, ProductDetailState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state.isLoading) {
              return const Center(child: CustomLoader());
            }

            if (state.errorMessage.isNotEmpty && state.product == null) {
              return Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.error_outline,
                      size: Dimens.standard_48,
                      color: AppColors.colorRed,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
                    Text(
                      state.errorMessage,
                      style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14,
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                    PrimaryButton(
                      width: Dimens.standard_120,
                      height: Dimens.standard_40,
                      text: AppStrings.retry,
                      onPressed: () => _cubit.fetchProductDetail(
                        ProductDetailRequest(id: widget.productId),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            }

            final product = state.product;
            if (product == null) {
              return const SizedBox.shrink();
            }

            final images =
                (product.images != null && product.images!.isNotEmpty)
                ? product.images!
                : (product.imageUrl != null ? [product.imageUrl!] : <String>[]);

            return Column(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: SingleChildScrollView(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        // Image Gallery
                        Container(
                          color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
                          width: double.infinity,
                          height: Dimens.standard_280,
                          child: Stack(
                            children: [
                              Center(
                                child: CustomNetworkImage(
                                  imageUrl: images.isNotEmpty
                                      ? images[state.selectedImageIndex.clamp(
                                          0,
                                          images.length - 1,
                                        )]
                                      : '',
                                  height: Dimens.standard_240,
                                  width: double.infinity,
                                  fit: BoxFit.contain,
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (product.dealBadge != null &&
                                  product.dealBadge!.isNotEmpty)
                                Positioned(
                                  top: Dimens.standard_16,
                                  left: Dimens.standard_16,
                                  child: Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      horizontal: Dimens.standard_10,
                                      vertical: Dimens.standard_4,
                                    ),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                                        Dimens.standard_6,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    child: Text(
                                      product.dealBadge!,
                                      style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12
                                          .copyWith(
                                            color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                                          ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),

                        // Image thumbnail strip
                        if (images.length > 1)
                          Container(
                            color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(
                              bottom: Dimens.standard_12,
                            ),
                            height: Dimens.standard_60,
                            child: ListView.builder(
                              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: Dimens.standard_16,
                              ),
                              itemCount: images.length,
                              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                                final isSelected =
                                    state.selectedImageIndex == index;
                                return GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () => _cubit.selectImage(index),
                                  child: Container(
                                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(
                                      right: Dimens.standard_8,
                                    ),
                                    width: Dimens.standard_50,
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      border: Border.all(
                                        color: isSelected
                                            ? AppColors.colorGreen
                                            : AppColors.colorGrey,
                                        width: isSelected
                                            ? Dimens.standard_2
                                            : Dimens.standard_1,
                                      ),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                                        Dimens.standard_8,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    child: ClipRRect(
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                                        Dimens.standard_6,
                                      ),
                                      child: CustomNetworkImage(
                                        imageUrl: images[index],
                                        fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                          ),

                        const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),

                        // Title & Price Section
                        Container(
                          width: double.infinity,
                          color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              if (product.categoryName != null &&
                                  product.categoryName!.isNotEmpty)
                                Text(
                                  '${product.categoryName} • ${product.subcategoryName ?? ""}',
                                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12
                                      .copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                                ),
                              const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_4),
                              Text(
                                product.name ?? '',
                                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
                                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_6),
                              Text(
                                product.packSize ?? '',
                                style: AppTextStyles.openSansSemiBold14
                                    .copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),
                              Row(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
                                textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    '₹${product.discountPrice ?? product.mrp ?? 0}',
                                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold24
                                        .copyWith(color: AppColors.colorGreen),
                                  ),
                                  if (product.mrp != null &&
                                      product.discountPrice != null &&
                                      product.mrp! >
                                          product.discountPrice!) ...[
                                    const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
                                    Text(
                                      '${AppStrings.mrp} ₹${product.mrp}',
                                      style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14
                                          .copyWith(
                                            color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                            decoration:
                                                TextDecoration.lineThrough,
                                          ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_8),
                                    Text(
                                      '${(((product.mrp! - product.discountPrice!) / product.mrp!) * 100).round()}% ${AppStrings.off}',
                                      style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12
                                          .copyWith(color: AppColors.colorRed),
                                    ),
                                  ],

==> lib/features/login/data/data_source/login_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/data/models/login_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/data/models/login_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/data/models/register_request.dart';

abstract class LoginRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, LoginResponse>> login({
    required LoginRequest user,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, LoginResponse>> register({
    required RegisterRequest user,
  });
}

class LoginRemoteDataSourceImpl implements LoginRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  LoginRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, LoginResponse>> login({
    required LoginRequest user,
  }) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.login,
        data: user.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold(
        (exception) {
          return Left(exception);
        },
        (response) {
          LoginResponse loginResponse = LoginResponse.fromJson(response.data);

          if (loginResponse.accessToken != null &&
              loginResponse.accessToken!.isNotEmpty) {
            networkService.updateHeader({
              'Authorization': 'Bearer ${loginResponse.accessToken}',
            });
          }

          return Right(loginResponse);
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'LoginRemoteDataSource.loginUser');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, LoginResponse>> register({
    required RegisterRequest user,
  }) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.register,
        data: user.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold(
        (exception) {
          return Left(exception);
        },
        (response) {
          LoginResponse loginResponse = LoginResponse.fromJson(response.data);

          if (loginResponse.accessToken != null &&
              loginResponse.accessToken!.isNotEmpty) {
            networkService.updateHeader({
              'Authorization': 'Bearer ${loginResponse.accessToken}',
            });
          }

          return Right(loginResponse);
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'LoginRemoteDataSource.registerUser',
      );
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/login/data/models/login_request.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

LoginRequest loginRequestFromJson(String str) =>
    LoginRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String loginRequestToJson(LoginRequest data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

class LoginRequest {
  final String? username;
  final String? password;

  const LoginRequest({required this.username, required this.password});

  factory LoginRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => LoginRequest(
    username: json["username"] ?? "",
    password: json["password"] ?? "",
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "username": username,
    "emailOrPhone": username,
    "password": password,
  };
}

==> lib/features/login/data/models/login_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

LoginResponse loginResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    LoginResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String loginResponseToJson(LoginResponse data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

class LoginResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final LoginData? data;

  const LoginResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  String? get accessToken => data?.accessToken;

  String? get refreshToken => data?.refreshToken;

  factory LoginResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => LoginResponse(
    status: json["status"] ?? false,
    message: json["message"] ?? "",
    data: json["data"] != null ? LoginData.fromJson(json["data"]) : null,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class LoginData {
  final String? accessToken;
  final String? refreshToken;
  final UserData? user;

  const LoginData({this.accessToken, this.refreshToken, this.user});

  factory LoginData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => LoginData(
    accessToken: json["accessToken"] ?? "",
    refreshToken: json["refreshToken"] ?? "",
    user: json["user"] != null ? UserData.fromJson(json["user"]) : null,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "accessToken": accessToken,
    "refreshToken": refreshToken,
    if (user != null) "user": user?.toJson(),
  };
}

class RegisterRequest {

  final String? phone;
  final String password;

  const RegisterRequest({

    required this.email,
    this.phone,

      RegisterRequest(

        email: json["email"] ?? "",
        phone: json["phone"] ?? "",
        password: json["password"] ?? "",
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "name": name,
    "email": email,
    if (phone != null && phone!.isNotEmpty) "phone": phone,
    "password": password,
  };
