==> lib/features/login/presentation/cubit/login_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/constant.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/database/hive_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/configuration.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/data/models/login_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/data/models/login_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/login/domain/use_case/login_use_case.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';

part 'login_state.dart';

/// LoginCubit - Handles all the login logic using the BLoC pattern.
///
/// What is a Cubit?
/// - A Cubit is a simpler version of BLoC (Business Logic Component)
/// - It manages "state" which is like a snapshot of the current situation
/// - When something changes, it "emits" a new state
/// - The UI listens to state changes and rebuilds automatically
///
/// What this cubit does:
/// 1. Validates username and password
/// 2. Makes API call to login
/// 3. Saves the user token to local storage
/// 4. Updates the network service with auth token
class LoginCubit extends Cubit<LoginState> {
  final LoginUseCases _loginUseCases;
  final HiveService _hiveService;
  final NetworkService _networkService;

  /// Constructor - Sets up the cubit with its dependencies
  /// - _loginUseCases: Contains the login API call logic
  /// - _hiveService: For saving data locally (like the auth token)
  /// - _networkService: For updating API headers with the token
  LoginCubit(this._loginUseCases)
    : _hiveService = GetIt.instance<HiveService>(),
      _networkService = GetIt.instance<NetworkService>(),
      super(const LoginState());

  /// Validates inputs and starts login if valid.
  ///
  /// How it works:
  /// 1. Check if username is empty -> show error
  /// 2. Check if password is empty -> show error
  /// 3. If both are valid -> create login request and call API
  void validate(String username, String password) {
    String usernameError = '';
    String passwordError = '';

    if (username.isEmpty) {
      usernameError = AppStrings.usernameValidation;
    }

    if (password.isEmpty) {
      passwordError = AppStrings.passwordValidation;
    }

    if (usernameError.isNotEmpty || passwordError.isNotEmpty) {
      // Has errors - show them
      emit(
        state.copyWith(
          usernameError: usernameError,
          passwordError: passwordError,
        ),
      );
    } else {
      // No errors - proceed with login
      LoginRequest request = LoginRequest(
        username: username,
        password: password,
      );
      login(request);
    }
  }

  /// Makes the API call to login the user.
  ///
  /// How it works:
  /// 1. Show loading state
  /// 2. Call the login API via use case
  /// 3. If error -> Show error message
  /// 4. If success -> Save token and user data, then show success
  Future<void> login(LoginRequest user) async {
    // Show loading spinner
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true));

    // Call the API
    Either<AppException, LoginResponse> result = await _loginUseCases.login(
      user: user,
    );

    // Handle the result
    result.fold(
      // This runs if there's an error
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('LoginCubit.login', error.identifier);
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            message: error.message,
            isLoading: false,
            isFailure: true,
            isSuccess: false,
          ),
        );
      },
      // This runs if successful
      (response) {
        // Extract UserData from the API response envelope
        final userData = response.data?.user ?? UserData();

        // Save user data to memory (for quick access)
        UserPreferences userPreferences = UserPreferences.instance;
        userPreferences.setUser(userData);

        // Save token to Hive (local database)
        String token = response.accessToken ?? '';
        String refToken = response.refreshToken ?? '';
        _hiveService.set(userToken, token);
        if (refToken.isNotEmpty) {
          _hiveService.set(refreshToken, refToken);
        }
        _hiveService.setUser(userData);

        // Add token to all future API requests
        _networkService.updateHeader({'Authorization': 'Bearer $token'});

        // Show success state
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoading: false,
            isSuccess: true,
            isFailure: false,
            loginData: response,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  /// Validates just the username field (called as user types)
  void validateUsername(String value) {
    String error = '';
    if (value.isEmpty) {
      error = AppStrings.usernameValidation;
    }
    emit(state.copyWith(usernameError: error));
  }

  /// Validates just the password field (called as user types)
  void validatePassword(String value) {
    String error = '';
    if (value.isEmpty) {
      error = AppStrings.passwordValidation;
    }
    emit(state.copyWith(passwordError: error));
  }

  /// Clears all state values back to default
  void clearState() {
    emit(
      state.copyWith(
        message: '',
        isFailure: false,
        isLoading: false,
        isSuccess: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Resets just the error state (after showing error toast)
  void resetError() {
    emit(state.copyWith(isFailure: false, message: ''));
  }
}

==> lib/features/login/presentation/cubit/login_state.dart <==
part of 'login_cubit.dart';

/// LoginState - Holds all the data for the login screen.
///
/// What is State?
/// - State is like a snapshot of the current situation
/// - It holds all the data the UI needs to display
/// - When state changes, the UI rebuilds to show the new data
///
/// Why use a single state class?
/// - Makes it easy to track what's happening
/// - All related data stays together
/// - Using copyWith() makes updating safe and clean
///
/// Properties:
/// - message: Error or success message
/// - usernameError: Error text for username field
/// - passwordError: Error text for password field
/// - isLoading: True when API call is in progress
/// - isFailure: True when login failed
/// - isSuccess: True when login succeeded
/// - loginData: The response from successful login
class LoginState extends Equatable {
  final String message;
  final String usernameError;
  final String passwordError;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool isFailure;
  final bool isSuccess;
  final LoginResponse? loginData;

  /// Constructor with default values
  /// All fields start empty or false by default
  const LoginState({
    this.message = '',
    this.usernameError = '',
    this.passwordError = '',
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.isFailure = false,
    this.isSuccess = false,
    this.loginData,
  });

  /// Creates a new state with some values changed.
  ///
  /// Example:
  /// state.copyWith(isLoading: true)
  /// This creates a new state where isLoading is true,
  /// but all other values stay the same.
  LoginState copyWith({
    String? message,
    String? usernameError,
    String? passwordError,
    bool? isLoading,
    bool? isFailure,
    bool? isSuccess,
    LoginResponse? loginData,
  }) {
    return LoginState(
      message: message ?? this.message,
      usernameError: usernameError ?? this.usernameError,
      passwordError: passwordError ?? this.passwordError,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      isFailure: isFailure ?? this.isFailure,
      isSuccess: isSuccess ?? this.isSuccess,
      loginData: loginData ?? this.loginData,
    );
  }

  /// Tells Equatable which fields to compare.
  /// Two states are equal if all these values are the same.
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    message,
    usernameError,
    passwordError,
    isLoading,
    isFailure,
    isSuccess,
    loginData,
  ];
}

==> lib/features/login/presentation/cubit/register_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

part 'register_state.dart';

class RegisterCubit extends Cubit<RegisterState> {
  final LoginUseCases _loginUseCases;
  final HiveService _hiveService;
  final NetworkService _networkService;

  RegisterCubit(this._loginUseCases)
    : _hiveService = GetIt.instance<HiveService>(),
      _networkService = GetIt.instance<NetworkService>(),
      super(const RegisterState());

  void validateAndRegister({
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String phone,
    required String password,
  }) {
    String nameError = '';
    String emailError = '';
    String passwordError = '';

    if (name.trim().isEmpty) {
      nameError = AppStrings.nameValidation;
    }

    if (email.trim().isEmpty || !email.contains('@')) {
      emailError = AppStrings.emailValidation;
    }

    if (password.length < 6) {
      passwordError = AppStrings.passwordLengthValidation;
    }

    if (nameError.isNotEmpty ||
        emailError.isNotEmpty ||
        passwordError.isNotEmpty) {
      emit(
        state.copyWith(
          nameError: nameError,
          emailError: emailError,
          passwordError: passwordError,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    emit(state.copyWith(nameError: '', emailError: '', passwordError: ''));
    register(
      RegisterRequest(
        name: name.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim().isNotEmpty ? phone.trim() : null,
        password: password,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> register(RegisterRequest request) async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, isFailure: false, isSuccess: false));

    final result = await _loginUseCases.register(user: request);

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        ErrorLogger.log('RegisterCubit.register', error.identifier);
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoading: false,
            isFailure: true,
            isSuccess: false,
            errorMessage: error.message,
          ),
        );
      },
      (response) {
        final userData = response.data?.user ?? UserData();

        // Save session in memory and encrypted Hive
        UserPreferences.instance.setUser(userData);
        final token = response.accessToken ?? '';
        final refToken = response.refreshToken ?? '';
        _hiveService.set(userToken, token);
        if (refToken.isNotEmpty) {
          _hiveService.set(refreshToken, refToken);
        }
        _hiveService.setUser(userData);
        _networkService.updateHeader({'Authorization': 'Bearer $token'});

        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoading: false,
            isSuccess: true,
            isFailure: false,
            registerData: response,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/login/presentation/cubit/register_state.dart <==
part of 'register_cubit.dart';

class RegisterState extends Equatable {
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool isSuccess;
  final bool isFailure;
  final String errorMessage;
  final String nameError;
  final String emailError;
  final String passwordError;
  final LoginResponse? registerData;

  const RegisterState({
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.isSuccess = false,
    this.isFailure = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.nameError = '',
    this.emailError = '',
    this.passwordError = '',
    this.registerData,
  });

  RegisterState copyWith({
    bool? isLoading,
    bool? isSuccess,
    bool? isFailure,
    String? errorMessage,
    String? nameError,
    String? emailError,
    String? passwordError,
    LoginResponse? registerData,
  }) {
    return RegisterState(
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      isSuccess: isSuccess ?? this.isSuccess,
      isFailure: isFailure ?? this.isFailure,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      nameError: nameError ?? this.nameError,
      emailError: emailError ?? this.emailError,
      passwordError: passwordError ?? this.passwordError,
      registerData: registerData ?? this.registerData,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    isLoading,
    isSuccess,
    isFailure,
    errorMessage,
    nameError,
    emailError,
    passwordError,
    registerData,
  ];
}

==> lib/features/orders/data/data_source/order_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_list_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_request.dart';

abstract class OrderRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderListResponse>> getOrders({
    OrderRequest? request,
  });

  Future<Either<AppException, OrderDetailResponse>> getOrderDetail(
    OrderDetailRequest request,
  );
}

class OrderRemoteDataSourceImpl implements OrderRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  OrderRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderListResponse>> getOrders({
    OrderRequest? request,
  }) async {
    try {
      final req = request ?? const OrderRequest();
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(
        ApiEndpoint.orders,
        queryParameters: req.toQueryParams(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = OrderListResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(e, 'OrderRemoteDataSource.getOrders');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderDetailResponse>> getOrderDetail(
    OrderDetailRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(
        '${ApiEndpoint.orders}/${request.orderId}',
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = OrderDetailResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'OrderRemoteDataSource.getOrderDetail',
      );
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_request.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

OrderDetailRequest orderDetailRequestFromJson(String str) =>
    OrderDetailRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String orderDetailRequestToJson(OrderDetailRequest data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class OrderDetailRequest {
  final String orderId;

  const OrderDetailRequest({
    required this.orderId,
  });

  factory OrderDetailRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      OrderDetailRequest(
        orderId: json["orderId"] ?? "",
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        "orderId": orderId,
      };
}

==> lib/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

OrderDetailResponse orderDetailResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    OrderDetailResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String orderDetailResponseToJson(OrderDetailResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class OrderDetailResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final OrderDetailData? data;

  const OrderDetailResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory OrderDetailResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      OrderDetailResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null
            ? OrderDetailData.fromJson(json["data"])
            : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class OrderDetailData {
  final String? id;
  final String? orderId;
  final String? orderNumber;
  final String? status;
  final String? orderStatus;
  final num? subtotal;
  final num? discount;
  final num? deliveryFee;
  final num? taxes;
  final num? grandTotal;
  final num? totalPayable;
  final String? paymentMethod;
  final String? paymentStatus;
  final String? deliverySlot;
  final String? estimatedDeliveryTime;
  final OrderDriver? driver;
  final String? createdAt;
  final List<OrderItemDetail>? items;
  final OrderDeliveryAddress? address;
  final List<OrderTimelineItem>? timeline;

  const OrderDetailData({
    this.id,
    this.orderId,
    this.orderNumber,
    this.status,
    this.orderStatus,
    this.subtotal,
    this.discount,
    this.deliveryFee,
    this.taxes,
    this.grandTotal,
    this.totalPayable,
    this.paymentMethod,
    this.paymentStatus,
    this.deliverySlot,
    this.estimatedDeliveryTime,
    this.driver,
    this.createdAt,
    this.items,
    this.address,
    this.timeline,
  });

  factory OrderDetailData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      OrderDetailData(
        id: json["id"] ?? "",
        orderId: json["orderId"] ?? "",
        orderNumber: json["orderNumber"] ?? "",
        status: (json["status"] ?? json["orderStatus"]) ?? "PLACED",
        orderStatus: (json["orderStatus"] ?? json["status"]) ?? "PLACED",
        subtotal: json["subtotal"] ?? 0,
        discount: json["discount"] ?? 0,
        deliveryFee: json["deliveryFee"] ?? 0,
        taxes: (json["taxes"] ?? json["tax"]) ?? 0,
        grandTotal: json["grandTotal"] ?? 0,
        totalPayable: (json["totalPayable"] ?? json["grandTotal"]) ?? 0,
        paymentMethod: json["paymentMethod"] ?? "COD",
        paymentStatus: json["paymentStatus"] ?? "PENDING",
        deliverySlot: json["deliverySlot"] ?? "",
        estimatedDeliveryTime: json["estimatedDeliveryTime"] ?? "",
        driver: json["driver"] != null
            ? OrderDriver.fromJson(json["driver"])
            : null,
        createdAt: json["createdAt"] ?? "",
        items: json["items"] != null
            ? List<OrderItemDetail>.from(
                json["items"].map((x) => OrderItemDetail.fromJson(x)),
              )
            : [],
        address: json["address"] != null
            ? OrderDeliveryAddress.fromJson(json["address"])
            : null,
        timeline: json["timeline"] != null
            ? List<OrderTimelineItem>.from(
                json["timeline"].map((x) => OrderTimelineItem.fromJson(x)),
              )
            : [],
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "id": id,
    "orderId": orderId,
    "orderNumber": orderNumber,
    "status": status,
    "orderStatus": orderStatus,
    "subtotal": subtotal,
    "discount": discount,
    "deliveryFee": deliveryFee,
    "taxes": taxes,
    "grandTotal": grandTotal,
    "totalPayable": totalPayable,
    "paymentMethod": paymentMethod,
    "paymentStatus": paymentStatus,
    "deliverySlot": deliverySlot,
    "estimatedDeliveryTime": estimatedDeliveryTime,
    if (driver != null) "driver": driver?.toJson(),
    "createdAt": createdAt,
    if (items != null)
      "items": List<dynamic>.from(items!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
    if (address != null) "address": address?.toJson(),
    if (timeline != null)
      "timeline": List<dynamic>.from(timeline!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
  };
}

import 'dart:convert';

OrderListResponse orderListResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    OrderListResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String orderListResponseToJson(OrderListResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class OrderListResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final OrderListData? data;

  const OrderListResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory OrderListResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      OrderListResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null
            ? OrderListData.fromJson(json["data"])
            : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class OrderListData {
  final List<OrderSummaryItem>? items;
  final int? totalCount;
  final int? page;

  const OrderListData({this.items, this.totalCount, this.page});

  factory OrderListData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => OrderListData(
    items: json["items"] != null
        ? List<OrderSummaryItem>.from(
            json["items"].map((x) => OrderSummaryItem.fromJson(x)),
          )
        : [],
    totalCount: json["totalCount"] ?? 0,
    page: json["page"] ?? 1,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (items != null)
      "items": List<dynamic>.from(items!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
    "totalCount": totalCount,
    "page": page,
  };
}

class OrderSummaryItem {
  final String? id;
  final String? orderId;
  final String? orderNumber;
  final int? itemCount;
  final num? grandTotal;
  final num? totalPayable;
  final String? paymentMethod;
  final String? paymentStatus;
  final String? status;
  final String? orderStatus;
  final String? estimatedDeliveryTime;
  final String? createdAt;

  const OrderSummaryItem({
    this.id,
    this.orderId,
    this.orderNumber,
    this.itemCount,
    this.grandTotal,
    this.totalPayable,
    this.paymentMethod,
    this.paymentStatus,
    this.status,
    this.orderStatus,
    this.estimatedDeliveryTime,
    this.createdAt,
  });

  factory OrderSummaryItem.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      OrderSummaryItem(
        id: json["id"] ?? "",
        orderId: json["orderId"] ?? "",
        orderNumber: json["orderNumber"] ?? "",
        itemCount: json["itemCount"] ?? 0,
        grandTotal: json["grandTotal"] ?? 0,
        totalPayable: (json["totalPayable"] ?? json["grandTotal"]) ?? 0,
        paymentMethod: json["paymentMethod"] ?? "COD",
        paymentStatus: json["paymentStatus"] ?? "PENDING",
        status: (json["status"] ?? json["orderStatus"]) ?? "PLACED",
        orderStatus: (json["orderStatus"] ?? json["status"]) ?? "PLACED",
        estimatedDeliveryTime: json["estimatedDeliveryTime"] ?? "",
        createdAt: json["createdAt"] ?? "",
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "id": id,
    "orderId": orderId,
    "orderNumber": orderNumber,
    "itemCount": itemCount,
    "grandTotal": grandTotal,
    "totalPayable": totalPayable,
    "paymentMethod": paymentMethod,
    "paymentStatus": paymentStatus,
    "status": status,
    "orderStatus": orderStatus,
    "estimatedDeliveryTime": estimatedDeliveryTime,
    "createdAt": createdAt,
  };
}

    OrderRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String orderRequestToJson(OrderRequest data) => json.encode(data.toJson());

class OrderRequest {
  final int page;
  final int limit;
  final String? status;

  const OrderRequest({this.page = 1, this.limit = pageLimit, this.status});

  factory OrderRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => OrderRequest(
    page: json["page"] ?? 1,
    limit: json["limit"] ?? pageLimit,
    status: json["status"] ?? "",
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "page": page,
    "limit": limit,
    if (status != null && status!.isNotEmpty) "status": status,
  };

  Map<String, dynamic> toQueryParams() => {
    "page": page,
    "limit": limit,
    if (status != null && status!.isNotEmpty) "status": status,
  };
}

class OrderRepositoryImpl implements OrderRepository {
  final OrderRemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  OrderRepositoryImpl(this.remoteDataSource);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderListResponse>> getOrders({
    OrderRequest? request,
  }) {
    return remoteDataSource.getOrders(request: request ?? const OrderRequest());
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, OrderDetailResponse>> getOrderDetail(
    OrderDetailRequest request,
  ) {
    return remoteDataSource.getOrderDetail(request);
  }
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';

    OrderRequest? request,

==> lib/features/orders/presentation/cubit/order_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_list_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/domain/use_case/order_use_case.dart';

part 'order_state.dart';

class OrderCubit extends Cubit<OrderState> {
  final OrderUseCase orderUseCase;

  OrderCubit(this.orderUseCase) : super(const OrderState());

  Future<void> fetchOrders({OrderRequest? request}) async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final result = await orderUseCase.getOrders(request: request);

    result.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (response) {
        final items = response.data?.items ?? [];
        emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, orders: items));
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> fetchOrderDetail(OrderDetailRequest request) async {
    emit(
      state.copyWith(
        isLoadingDetail: true,
        detailErrorMessage: '',
        selectedOrderDetail: null,
      ),
    );

    final result = await orderUseCase.getOrderDetail(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) => emit(
        state.copyWith(
          isLoadingDetail: false,
          detailErrorMessage: error.message,
        ),
      ),
      (response) {
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isLoadingDetail: false,
            selectedOrderDetail: response.data,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/orders/presentation/cubit/order_state.dart <==
part of 'order_cubit.dart';

class OrderState extends Equatable {
  final bool isLoading;
  final String errorMessage;
  final List<OrderSummaryItem> orders;
  final bool isLoadingDetail;
  final String detailErrorMessage;
  final OrderDetailData? selectedOrderDetail;

  const OrderState({
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.orders = const [],
    this.isLoadingDetail = false,
    this.detailErrorMessage = '',
    this.selectedOrderDetail,
  });

  OrderState copyWith({
    bool? isLoading,
    String? errorMessage,
    List<OrderSummaryItem>? orders,
    bool? isLoadingDetail,
    String? detailErrorMessage,
    OrderDetailData? selectedOrderDetail,
  }) {
    return OrderState(
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      orders: orders ?? this.orders,
      isLoadingDetail: isLoadingDetail ?? this.isLoadingDetail,
      detailErrorMessage: detailErrorMessage ?? this.detailErrorMessage,
      selectedOrderDetail: selectedOrderDetail ?? this.selectedOrderDetail,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    isLoading,
    errorMessage,
    orders,
    isLoadingDetail,
    detailErrorMessage,
    selectedOrderDetail,
  ];
}

==> lib/features/orders/presentation/pages/order_details_page.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/dependency_injection/injector.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/data/models/order_detail_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/domain/use_case/order_use_case.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/orders/presentation/cubit/order_cubit.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/config/dimens.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/theme/text_styles.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/widgets/custom_loader.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/widgets/primary_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

class OrderDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String orderId;

  const OrderDetailPage({super.key, required this.orderId});

  @override
  State<OrderDetailPage> createState() => _OrderDetailPageState();
}

class _OrderDetailPageState extends State<OrderDetailPage> {
  late OrderCubit _orderCubit;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _orderCubit = OrderCubit(injector<OrderUseCase>())
      ..fetchOrderDetail(OrderDetailRequest(orderId: widget.orderId));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _orderCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _orderCubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              size: Dimens.standard_20,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          title: Text(
            AppStrings.trackOrder,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<OrderCubit, OrderState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state.isLoadingDetail) {
              return const Center(child: CustomLoader());
            }

            if (state.detailErrorMessage.isNotEmpty) {
              return Center(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_24),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        state.detailErrorMessage,
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                          color: AppColors.colorRed,
                        ),
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                      PrimaryButton(
                        text: AppStrings.retry,
                        onPressed: () => _orderCubit.fetchOrderDetail(
                          OrderDetailRequest(orderId: widget.orderId),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }

            final order = state.selectedOrderDetail;
            if (order == null) {
              return const SizedBox.shrink();
            }

            return ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
              children: [
                // Order Header Card
                _buildHeaderCard(order),
                const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),

                // Order Timeline
                if (order.timeline != null && order.timeline!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  _buildTimelineCard(order.timeline!),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                ],

                // Driver Info
                if (order.driver != null &&
                    order.driver?.name?.isNotEmpty == true) ...[
                  _buildDriverCard(order.driver!),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                ],

                // Items list
                if (order.items != null && order.items!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  _buildItemsCard(order.items!),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                ],

                // Delivery Address
                if (order.address != null) ...[
                  _buildAddressCard(order.address!),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
                ],

                // Bill Breakdown
                _buildBillCard(order),
                const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_24),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeaderCard(OrderDetailData order) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  order.orderNumber ?? 'Order #${widget.orderId}',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: Dimens.standard_10,
                  vertical: Dimens.standard_4,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.colorGreen.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_6),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  order.status ?? 'PLACED',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
          Text(
            order.estimatedDeliveryTime?.isNotEmpty == true
                ? '${AppStrings.estimatedDelivery}: ${order.estimatedDeliveryTime}'
                : '${AppStrings.estimatedDelivery}: 15-20 mins',
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTimelineCard(List<OrderTimelineItem> timeline) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            AppStrings.orderTimeline,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_16),
          ...List.generate(timeline.length, (index) {
            final item = timeline[index];
            final isDone = item.completed == true;
            final isLast = index == timeline.length - 1;

            return Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Column(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: Dimens.standard_24,
                      height: Dimens.standard_24,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: isDone
                            ? AppColors.colorGreen
                            : AppColors.cardBackground,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        border: Border.all(
                          color: isDone
                              ? AppColors.colorGreen
                              : AppColors.dividerColor,
                          width: 2,
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: isDone
                          ? const Icon(
                              Icons.check,
                              size: Dimens.standard_14,
                              color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                            )
                          : null,
                    ),
                    if (!isLast)
                      Container(
                        width: 2,
                        height: Dimens.standard_32,
                        color: isDone
                            ? AppColors.colorGreen
                            : AppColors.dividerColor,
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: Dimens.standard_2),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          item.label ?? item.status ?? '',
                          style: isDone
                              ? AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                )
                              : AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                ),
                        ),
                        if (item.timestamp != null &&
                            item.timestamp!.isNotEmpty)
                          Text(
                            item.timestamp!,
                            style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular10.copyWith(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDriverCard(OrderDriver driver) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: Dimens.standard_48,
            height: Dimens.standard_48,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.colorGreen.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.delivery_dining,
              color: AppColors.colorGreen,
              size: Dimens.standard_28,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: Dimens.standard_16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  AppStrings.deliveryPartner,
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular12.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  driver.name ?? 'Delivery Partner',
                  style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
                if (driver.vehicle != null && driver.vehicle!.isNotEmpty)
                  Text(
                    driver.vehicle!,

  Widget _buildRow(
    String label,

    Color? valueColor,
  }) {

          style: isBold

              : AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
        ),
        Text(
          value,
          style: isBold
              ? AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                )
              : AppTextStyles.openSansBold14.copyWith(
                  color: valueColor ?? AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Color _getStatusColor(String status) {
    switch (status.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'DELIVERED':
        return AppColors.colorGreen;
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return AppColors.colorRed;
      case 'OUT_FOR_DELIVERY':
        return const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
      default:
        return const Color(0xFF2563EB);
    }
  }

==> lib/features/profile/data/data_source/profile_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/profile/data/models/profile_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/profile/data/models/update_profile_request.dart';

abstract class ProfileRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, ProfileResponse>> getProfile();

  Future<Either<AppException, ProfileResponse>> updateProfile(
    UpdateProfileRequest request,
  );
}

class ProfileRemoteDataSourceImpl implements ProfileRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  ProfileRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProfileResponse>> getProfile() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(ApiEndpoint.profile);
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = ProfileResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'ProfileRemoteDataSource.getProfile',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ProfileResponse>> updateProfile(
    UpdateProfileRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.put(
        ApiEndpoint.profile,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = ProfileResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'ProfileRemoteDataSource.updateProfile',
      );
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/profile/data/models/profile_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

ProfileResponse profileResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    ProfileResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String profileResponseToJson(ProfileResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class ProfileResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final UserData? data;

  const ProfileResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory ProfileResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      ProfileResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null ? UserData.fromJson(json["data"]) : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

==> lib/features/profile/data/models/update_profile_request.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

UpdateProfileRequest updateProfileRequestFromJson(String str) =>
    UpdateProfileRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String updateProfileRequestToJson(UpdateProfileRequest data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class UpdateProfileRequest {
  final String? name;
  final String? phone;
  final String? avatarUrl;

  const UpdateProfileRequest({this.name, this.phone, this.avatarUrl});

  factory UpdateProfileRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      UpdateProfileRequest(
        name: json["name"] ?? "",
        phone: json["phone"] ?? "",
        avatarUrl: json["avatarUrl"] ?? "",
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (name != null) "name": name,
    if (phone != null) "phone": phone,
    if (avatarUrl != null) "avatarUrl": avatarUrl,
  };
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';

  ProfileUseCase(this.repository);

  Future<Either<AppException, ProfileResponse>> getProfile() {

==> lib/features/profile/presentation/cubit/profile_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/configuration.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/profile/data/models/update_profile_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/profile/domain/use_case/profile_use_case.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/user_data.dart';

part 'profile_state.dart';

class ProfileCubit extends Cubit<ProfileState> {
  final ProfileUseCase profileUseCase;

  ProfileCubit(this.profileUseCase)
    : super(ProfileState(user: UserPreferences.instance.getUser()));

  Future<void> fetchProfile() async {
    final hasCached = state.user != null;
    if (!hasCached) {
      emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));
    }

    final result = await profileUseCase.getProfile();

    result.fold(
      (error) => emit(
        state.copyWith(
          isLoading: false,
          errorMessage: hasCached ? '' : error.message,
        ),
      ),
      (response) {
        if (response.data != null) {
          UserPreferences.instance.setUser(response.data!);
        }
        emit(
          state.copyWith(isLoading: false, user: response.data ?? state.user),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void toggleEditMode([bool? force]) {
    emit(
      state.copyWith(
        isEditing: force ?? !state.isEditing,
        updateSuccess: false,
        errorMessage: '',
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> updateProfile(UpdateProfileRequest request) async {
    emit(
      state.copyWith(isUpdating: true, errorMessage: '', updateSuccess: false),
    );

    final result = await profileUseCase.updateProfile(request);

    result.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isUpdating: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (response) {
        if (response.data != null) {
          UserPreferences.instance.setUser(response.data!);
        }
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            isUpdating: false,
            isEditing: false,
            updateSuccess: true,
            user: response.data ?? state.user,
            successMessage: response.message ?? 'Profile updated successfully',
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/profile/presentation/cubit/profile_state.dart <==
part of 'profile_cubit.dart';

class ProfileState extends Equatable {
  final bool isLoading;
  final String errorMessage;
  final UserData? user;
  final bool isEditing;
  final bool isUpdating;
  final bool updateSuccess;
  final String successMessage;

  const ProfileState({
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.errorMessage = '',
    this.user,
    this.isEditing = false,
    this.isUpdating = false,
    this.updateSuccess = false,
    this.successMessage = '',
  });

  ProfileState copyWith({
    bool? isLoading,
    String? errorMessage,
    UserData? user,
    bool? isEditing,
    bool? isUpdating,
    bool? updateSuccess,
    String? successMessage,
  }) {
    return ProfileState(
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
      user: user ?? this.user,
      isEditing: isEditing ?? this.isEditing,
      isUpdating: isUpdating ?? this.isUpdating,
      updateSuccess: updateSuccess ?? this.updateSuccess,
      successMessage: successMessage ?? this.successMessage,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    isLoading,
    errorMessage,
    user,
    isEditing,
    isUpdating,
    updateSuccess,
    successMessage,
  ];

class _ProfilePageState extends State<ProfilePage> {
  late ProfileCubit _profileCubit;
  final TextEditingController _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _phoneController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _profileCubit = ProfileCubit(injector<ProfileUseCase>())..fetchProfile();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _profileCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _syncControllersWithUser() {
    final user = _profileCubit.state.user;
    if (user != null) {
      _nameController.text = user.name ?? '';
      _phoneController.text = user.phone ?? '';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _profileCubit,
      child: BlocConsumer<ProfileCubit, ProfileState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          if (state.updateSuccess) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(AppStrings.profileUpdatedSuccess),
                backgroundColor: AppColors.colorGreen,
              ),
            );
          }
          if (state.errorMessage.isNotEmpty) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(state.errorMessage),
                backgroundColor: AppColors.colorRed,
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          final user = state.user;
          final isEditing = state.isEditing;

          return Scaffold(
            backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
            appBar: AppBar(
              backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
              elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
              leading: IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(
                  Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  size: Dimens.standard_20,
                ),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
              ),
              title: Text(
                AppStrings.myProfile,
                style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            body: state.isLoading && user == null
                ? const Center(child: CustomLoader())
                : ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
                    children: [
                      // Header Card with Avatar and Basic Name
                      _buildAvatarHeader(user),
                      const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_20),

                      // Content Switch: View Mode vs Edit Mode
                      if (isEditing)
                        _buildEditMode(context, state)
                      else
                        _buildViewMode(context, user),
                    ],
                  ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAvatarHeader(UserData? user) {
    final avatarUrl = user?.avatarUrl;
    final name = user?.name ?? 'Grocery Shopper';
    final email = user?.email ?? '';

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.dividerColor),

        children: [
          Container(
            width: Dimens.standard_80,
            height: Dimens.standard_80,
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(shape: BoxShape.circle),
            clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
            child: avatarUrl != null && avatarUrl.isNotEmpty
                ? CustomNetworkImage(
                    imageUrl: avatarUrl,
                    width: Dimens.standard_80,
                    height: Dimens.standard_80,
                    fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  )
                : Container(
                    color: AppColors.colorGreen.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    child: Center(
                      child: Text(
                        (name.isNotEmpty ? name[0] : 'U').toUpperCase(),
                        style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold30.copyWith(
                          color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_12),

            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),

              email,
              style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular14.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildViewMode(BuildContext context, UserData? user) {

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/app_strings.dart';

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/theme/text_styles.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/widgets/custom_loader.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

class SplashScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SplashScreen> createState() => _SplashScreenState();
}

class _SplashScreenState extends State<SplashScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> _fadeAnimation;
  late Animation<double> _scaleAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _setupAnimations();
    _checkAuthAndNavigate();
  }

  void _setupAnimations() {
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1500),
    );

    _fadeAnimation =
        Tween<double>(begin: Dimens.standard_0, end: Dimens.standard_1).animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _animationController,
            curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.6, curve: Curves.easeOut),
          ),
        );

    _scaleAnimation =
        Tween<double>(begin: Dimens.decimal_80, end: Dimens.standard_1).animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _animationController,
            curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.6, curve: Curves.elasticOut),
          ),
        );

    _animationController.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _checkAuthAndNavigate() async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));
    if (!mounted) return;

    final hiveService = GetIt.instance<HiveService>();
    final token = await hiveService.get(userToken);

    if (token != null && token.toString().isNotEmpty) {
      final networkService = GetIt.instance<NetworkService>();
      networkService.updateHeader({'Authorization': 'Bearer $token'});
      if (mounted) context.go(RoutesName.homePage);
    } else {
      if (mounted) context.go(RoutesName.loginPath);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
            colors: [AppColors.colorPrimary, AppColors.colorPrimaryDark],
          ),
        ),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            // Decorative background pattern (circles)
            Positioned(
              top: -Dimens.standard_100,
              right: -Dimens.standard_100,
              child: Container(
                width: Dimens.standard_300,
                height: Dimens.standard_300,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  color: AppColors.colorWhite.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Positioned(
              bottom: -Dimens.standard_50,
              left: -Dimens.standard_50,
              child: Container(
                width: Dimens.standard_200,
                height: Dimens.standard_200,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  color: AppColors.colorWhite.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                ),
              ),
            ),

            Center(
              child: AnimatedBuilder(
                animation: _animationController,
                builder: (context, child) {
                  return FadeTransition(
                    opacity: _fadeAnimation,
                    child: ScaleTransition(
                      scale: _scaleAnimation,
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          const Icon(
                            Icons.shopping_basket_rounded,
                            size: Dimens.standard_100,
                            color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_24),

                          // App name text
                          Text(
                            AppStrings.appName,
                            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold40.copyWith(
                              color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                              letterSpacing:
                                  Dimens.standard_1 + Dimens.decimal_20,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),

                          // Tagline text
                          Text(
                            AppStrings.appSubtitle,
                            style: AppTextStyles.openSansRegular16.copyWith(
                              color: AppColors.colorWhite.withValues(
                                alpha: 0.9,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_60),

                          const CustomLoader(
                            size: Dimens.standard_32,
                            strokeWidth: Dimens.standard_3,
                            isCentered: false,
                            color: AppColors.colorWhite,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/data/data_source/wishlist_remote_data_source.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/constants/endpoints.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/network_service.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/utils/error_logger.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/wishlist_response.dart';

abstract class WishlistRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Either<AppException, WishlistResponse>> getWishlist();

  Future<Either<AppException, ToggleWishlistResponse>> toggleWishlist(
    ToggleWishlistRequest request,
  );
}

class WishlistRemoteDataSourceImpl implements WishlistRemoteDataSource {
  final NetworkService networkService;

  WishlistRemoteDataSourceImpl(this.networkService);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, WishlistResponse>> getWishlist() async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.get(ApiEndpoint.wishlist);
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = WishlistResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'WishlistRemoteDataSource.getWishlist',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ToggleWishlistResponse>> toggleWishlist(
    ToggleWishlistRequest request,
  ) async {
    try {
      Either eitherType = await networkService.post(
        ApiEndpoint.wishlistToggle,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return eitherType.fold((exception) => Left(exception), (response) {
        final res = ToggleWishlistResponse.fromJson(response.data);
        return Right(res);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return ErrorLogger.handleException(
        e,
        'WishlistRemoteDataSource.toggleWishlist',
      );
    }
  }
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_request.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

ToggleWishlistRequest toggleWishlistRequestFromJson(String str) =>
    ToggleWishlistRequest.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String toggleWishlistRequestToJson(ToggleWishlistRequest data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class ToggleWishlistRequest {
  final String? productId;

  const ToggleWishlistRequest({this.productId});

  factory ToggleWishlistRequest.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      ToggleWishlistRequest(productId: json["productId"]);

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (productId != null) "productId": productId,
  };
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';

ToggleWishlistResponse toggleWishlistResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    ToggleWishlistResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String toggleWishlistResponseToJson(ToggleWishlistResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class ToggleWishlistResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final ToggleWishlistData? data;

  const ToggleWishlistResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory ToggleWishlistResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      ToggleWishlistResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null
            ? ToggleWishlistData.fromJson(json["data"])
            : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class ToggleWishlistData {
  final bool? inWishlist;
  final String? productId;

  const ToggleWishlistData({this.inWishlist, this.productId});

  factory ToggleWishlistData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      ToggleWishlistData(
        inWishlist: json["inWishlist"] ?? false,
        productId: json["productId"] ?? "",
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "inWishlist": inWishlist,
    "productId": productId,
  };
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/data/models/wishlist_response.dart <==
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_model.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/shared/models/page_meta.dart';

WishlistResponse wishlistResponseFromJson(String str) =>
    WishlistResponse.fromJson(json.decode(str));

String wishlistResponseToJson(WishlistResponse data) =>
    json.encode(data.toJson());

class WishlistResponse {
  final bool? status;
  final String? message;
  final WishlistData? data;

  const WishlistResponse({this.status, this.message, this.data});

  factory WishlistResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      WishlistResponse(
        status: json["status"] ?? false,
        message: json["message"] ?? "",
        data: json["data"] != null ? WishlistData.fromJson(json["data"]) : null,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    "status": status,
    "message": message,
    if (data != null) "data": data?.toJson(),
  };
}

class WishlistData {
  final List<ProductModel>? products;
  final PageMeta? pageMeta;

  const WishlistData({this.products, this.pageMeta});

  factory WishlistData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => WishlistData(
    products: json["products"] != null
        ? List<ProductModel>.from(
            json["products"].map((x) => ProductModel.fromJson(x)),
          )
        : [],
    pageMeta: json["pageMeta"] != null
        ? PageMeta.fromJson(json["pageMeta"])
        : null,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    if (products != null)
      "products": List<dynamic>.from(products!.map((x) => x.toJson())),
    if (pageMeta != null) "pageMeta": pageMeta?.toJson(),
  };
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';

class WishlistRepositoryImpl implements WishlistRepository {
  final WishlistRemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  WishlistRepositoryImpl(this.remoteDataSource);

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, WishlistResponse>> getWishlist() {
    return remoteDataSource.getWishlist();
  }

  @override
  Future<Either<AppException, ToggleWishlistResponse>> toggleWishlist(
    ToggleWishlistRequest request,
  ) {
    return remoteDataSource.toggleWishlist(request);
  }
}

import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_request.dart';

abstract class WishlistRepository {
  Future<Either<AppException, WishlistResponse>> getWishlist();

  Future<Either<AppException, ToggleWishlistResponse>> toggleWishlist(
    ToggleWishlistRequest request,
  );
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/domain/use_case/wishlist_use_case.dart <==
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/exceptions/http_exception.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/core/network/model/either.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/wishlist_response.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/domain/repositories/wishlist_repository.dart';

class WishlistUseCase {
  final WishlistRepository repository;

  WishlistUseCase(this.repository);

  Future<Either<AppException, WishlistResponse>> getWishlist() {
    return repository.getWishlist();
  }

  Future<Either<AppException, ToggleWishlistResponse>> toggleWishlist(
    ToggleWishlistRequest request,
  ) {
    return repository.toggleWishlist(request);
  }
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/presentation/cubit/wishlist_cubit.dart <==
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/grocery/data/models/product_model.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/data/models/toggle_wishlist_request.dart';
import 'package:gear_up_bloc/features/wishlist/domain/use_case/wishlist_use_case.dart';

part 'wishlist_state.dart';

class WishlistCubit extends Cubit<WishlistState> {
  final WishlistUseCase _wishlistUseCase;

  WishlistCubit(this._wishlistUseCase) : super(const WishlistState());

  Future<void> fetchWishlist() async {
    emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: true, errorMessage: ''));

    final result = await _wishlistUseCase.getWishlist();

    result.fold(
      (error) =>
          emit(state.copyWith(isLoading: false, errorMessage: error.message)),
      (response) {
        final products = response.data?.products ?? [];
        final ids = products
            .map((p) => p.id ?? '')
            .where((id) => id.isNotEmpty)
            .toSet();

        emit(
          state.copyWith(isLoading: false, items: products, wishlistIds: ids),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> toggleWishlist(ProductModel product) async {
    final productId = product.id ?? '';
    if (productId.isEmpty) return;

    final wasInWishlist = state.isInWishlist(productId);
    final updatedIds = Set<String>.from(state.wishlistIds);

    if (wasInWishlist) {
      updatedIds.remove(productId);
    } else {
      updatedIds.add(productId);
    }

    // Optimistic UI update
    emit(state.copyWith(wishlistIds: updatedIds, togglingId: productId));

    final result = await _wishlistUseCase.toggleWishlist(
      ToggleWishlistRequest(productId: productId),
    );

    result.fold(
      (error) {
        // Revert on error
        final revertedIds = Set<String>.from(state.wishlistIds);
        if (wasInWishlist) {
          revertedIds.add(productId);
        } else {
          revertedIds.remove(productId);
        }
        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            wishlistIds: revertedIds,
            togglingId: null,
            errorMessage: error.message,
          ),
        );
      },
      (response) {
        final inWishlist = response.data?.inWishlist ?? false;
        final syncedIds = Set<String>.from(state.wishlistIds);
        List<ProductModel> updatedItems = List.from(state.items);

        if (inWishlist) {
          syncedIds.add(productId);
          if (!updatedItems.any((p) => p.id == productId)) {
            updatedItems.add(product);
          }
        } else {
          syncedIds.remove(productId);
          updatedItems.removeWhere((p) => p.id == productId);
        }

        emit(
          state.copyWith(
            wishlistIds: syncedIds,
            items: updatedItems,
            togglingId: null,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

==> lib/features/wishlist/presentation/cubit/wishlist_state.dart <==
part of 'wishlist_cubit.dart';

class WishlistState extends Equatable {
  final List<ProductModel> items;
  final Set<String> wishlistIds;
  final bool isLoading;
  final String? togglingId;
  final String errorMessage;

  const WishlistState({
    this.items = const [],
    this.wishlistIds = const {},
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.togglingId,
    this.errorMessage = '',
  });

  WishlistState copyWith({
    List<ProductModel>? items,
    Set<String>? wishlistIds,
    bool? isLoading,
    String? togglingId,
    String? errorMessage,
  }) {
    return WishlistState(
      items: items ?? this.items,
      wishlistIds: wishlistIds ?? this.wishlistIds,
      isLoading: isLoading ?? this.isLoading,
      togglingId: togglingId,
      errorMessage: errorMessage ?? this.errorMessage,
    );
  }

  bool isInWishlist(String? productId) {
    if (productId == null) return false;
    return wishlistIds.contains(productId);
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    items,
    wishlistIds,
    isLoading,
    togglingId,
    errorMessage,
  ];
}

class WishlistPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const WishlistPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<WishlistPage> createState() => _WishlistPageState();

class _WishlistPageState extends State<WishlistPage> {
  late WishlistCubit _wishlistCubit;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _wishlistCubit = WishlistCubit(injector<WishlistUseCase>())
      ..fetchWishlist();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _wishlistCubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => _wishlistCubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.appBackGround,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceColor,
          elevation: Dimens.standard_0,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              size: Dimens.standard_20,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          title: Text(
            AppStrings.myWishlist,
            style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold18.copyWith(
              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.shopping_cart_outlined,
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
              ),
              onPressed: () => context.push(RoutesName.cart),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<WishlistCubit, WishlistState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state.isLoading) {
              return const Center(child: CustomLoader());
            }

            if (state.items.isEmpty) {
              return _buildEmptyWishlist(context);
            }

            return GridView.builder(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_16),
              gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                crossAxisCount: 2,
                childAspectRatio: 0.7,
                crossAxisSpacing: Dimens.standard_16,
                mainAxisSpacing: Dimens.standard_16,
              ),
              itemCount: state.items.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final item = state.items[index];
                return _buildWishlistCard(context, item);
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildWishlistCard(BuildContext context, ProductModel item) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () {
        if (item.id != null && item.id!.isNotEmpty) {
          context.push(RoutesName.productDetail, extra: item.id);
        }
      },
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppColors.cardBackground,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: AppColors.colorBlack.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
              blurRadius: Dimens.standard_10,
              offset: const Offset(Dimens.standard_0, Dimens.standard_5),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                      top: Radius.circular(Dimens.standard_12),
                    ),
                    child: CustomNetworkImage(
                      imageUrl: item.imageUrl ?? '',
                      width: double.infinity,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    top: Dimens.standard_6,
                    right: Dimens.standard_6,
                    child: CircleAvatar(
                      radius: Dimens.standard_16,
                      backgroundColor: AppColors.colorWhite.withValues(
                        alpha: 0.9,
                      ),
                      child: IconButton(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        icon: const Icon(
                          Icons.favorite,
                          color: AppColors.colorRed,
                          size: Dimens.standard_18,
                        ),
                        onPressed: () => _wishlistCubit.toggleWishlist(item),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_12),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    item.name ?? '',
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold14,
                    maxLines: 2,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_4),
                  Text(
                    '₹${item.discountPrice ?? item.mrp ?? 0}',
                    style: AppTextStyles.openSansBold16.copyWith(
                      color: AppColors.colorGreen,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Dimens.standard_8),
                  PrimaryButton(
                    text: AppStrings.add,
                    isOutlined: true,
                    height: Dimens.standard_36,
                    borderRadius: Dimens.standard_8,
                    textStyle: AppTextStyles.openSansBold12,
                    onPressed: () {
                      if (item.id != null && item.id!.isNotEmpty) {
                        injector<CartUseCase>().addItem(
                          AddToCartRequest(productId: item.id!, quantity: 1),
                        );
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          SnackBar(
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.colorGreen,
                            content: Text('${item.name} ${AppStrings.add}!'),
                            duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
                          ),
                        );
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyWishlist(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Dimens.standard_24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(
              Icons.favorite_border_rounded,

            ),
